minute' explicitly and LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS already defaults to 60. Assumed: LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED=false disables per-key limiting too, because the existing tests and README present that flag as the single kill switch for throttling. Assumed: for a known key the key quota replaces the per-IP creation limit rather than stacking with it, because the requirement says such a request 'is limited against that key's quota' and stacking would make any quota above LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX unreachable. Assumed: each entry is split on the last ':' so the left side is the key name and the right side an integer quota; entries that do not parse, have an empty name, or have a quota <= 0 are logged at WARNING (name and value redacted) and skipped, following the fail-soft pattern in app/config.py. Assumed: the X-API-Key value is read at most 256 characters and compared exactly; a hash of the key (per-boot salt, SHA-256, bucket prefix 'create-key') is what enters the limiter map, so the key value never leaves the request scope. Assumed: the 429 keeps the existing stable error code 'rate_limited' rather than introducing a new one, because clients already match on it. No branch of this flow reaches app/db.py, the links or clicks tables, the uvicorn access log, or any response body; the click-recording path in redirect_to_target is untouched.", "raw_requirement": "Add per-API-key rate limiting to the existing URL shortener. Keys come from the environment (SHORTENER_API_KEYS, comma-separated key:quota pairs), quota is creations per minute, presented via X-API-Key; unknown/absent key falls back to the existing per-IP limit; over-quota returns 429 with Retry-After in whole seconds; additive, no schema change, no admin surface, redirect per-IP throttle untouched.", "scenario": "brownfield"}, "prompt": "REQUIREMENT\nAdd per-API-key rate limiting to the existing URL shortener.\n\nKeys come from the environment, not from the database. The variable SHORTENER_API_KEYS holds a comma-separated list of key:quota pairs, so a key named alpha with a quota of 100 and a key named beta with a quota of 20 are written as two such pairs. The quota is requests per minute for link creation. There is no admin surface and no schema change; an unknown key is treated as no key.\n\nA caller presents its key in an X-API-Key header. A request carrying a known key is limited against that key's quota. A request with no key, or an unknown one, keeps the existing per-IP limit exactly as it behaves today, so existing clients continue to work unchanged.\n\nA caller over its quota receives HTTP 429 with a Retry-After header giving whole seconds until the window resets.\n\nThis change is additive. The existing per-IP throttle on redirects stays exactly as it is; do not remove or weaken it. Per-key limiting applies only to link creation, and redirects are never limited by key.\n\nEXISTING CODE\n--- README.md ---\n# URL Shortener\n\nPublic JSON API that turns a caller supplied public `http(s)` URL into an\nunguessable base62 short code, redirects visitors until the link expires and\nexposes per-link click analytics that contain no visitor IP addresses.\n\n## Run\n\n```bash\npip install -r requirements.txt\nuvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000\n```\n\n## Endpoints\n\n| Method | Path | Purpose |\n| ------ | ---- | ------- |\n| POST | `/api/links` | Create a short link (`{\"url\": \"...\", \"expires_at\": \"...\"}`) |\n| GET | `/api/links/{code}/stats` | Click analytics (`limit`, `offset`) |\n| GET | `/health` | Liveness; `{\"status\":\"ok\"}` or 503 `{\"status\":\"degraded\"}` |\n| GET | `/{code}` | 307 redirect to the stored destination |\n\nErrors always use `{\"error\": {\"code\": \"...\", \"message\": \"...\"}}`.\n\n## Configuration\n\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n| -------- | ------- | ------- |\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite database file |\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` |\n| `LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS` | `30` | Default link lifetime |\n| `LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH` | `2048` | Maximum accepted URL length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert attempts on code collision |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Creations per client per window |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Rolling window length |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | `false`/`0`/`no` disables the limiter |\n| `LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR` | `false` | Use first `X-Forwarded-For` entry |\n| `LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT` | `50` | Default clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT` | `500` | Maximum clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED` | `true` | Resolve and denylist-check hosts |\n| `LINKS_LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\n\nNo secrets are read or stored by the service.\n\n## Security notes\n\n* Scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`) plus an address denylist covering\n loopback, private, link-local (including `169.254.169.254`), unique-local,\n CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and reserved ranges, with IPv4-mapped,\n IPv4-compatible, 6to4, Teredo and decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals decoded.\n* The service never performs an outbound HTTP request to a destination.\n* Redirect targets come only from the validated, stored row.\n* Rate limiting keys on a per-boot salted hash of the client address; no IP is\n written to the database, to a file or to a log.\n\n\n--- app/__init__.py ---\n\"\"\"URL shortener service package.\n\nExposes :func:`app.main.create_app`, the application factory, and the module\nlevel ``app`` ASGI application used by uvicorn.\n\"\"\"\n\n__all__ = [\"__version__\"]\n\n__version__ = \"1.0.0\"\n\n\n--- app/codes.py ---\n\"\"\"Short code generation.\n\nCodes are drawn from a cryptographically secure random source; they are never\nderived from a database row id or any other sequence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport string\n\nALPHABET = string.ascii_uppercase + string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits\n\n\ndef generate_code(length: int) -> str:\n \"\"\"Generate a base62 short code.\n\n ``length`` is the number of characters to produce. Returns a string of\n exactly ``length`` characters drawn uniformly from ``[A-Za-z0-9]`` using\n :mod:`secrets`. Raises :class:`ValueError` when ``length`` is not positive.\n \"\"\"\n if length <= 0:\n raise ValueError(\"code length must be positive\")\n return \"\".join(secrets.choice(ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\n--- app/config.py ---\n\"\"\"Environment driven configuration for the URL shortener service.\n\nAll settings come from environment variables with the exact names and defaults\nfixed by the design document. No secrets are read or stored here.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Mapping, Optional\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.config\")\n\n#: Hard ceiling applied to the accepted ``url`` length regardless of\n#: ``LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH``. It bounds the string that ever reaches the\n#: database and the pydantic request model.\nABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH = 8192\n\n#: Hard ceiling for the client supplied ``expires_at`` string.\nMAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH = 64\n\n#: Hard ceiling for ``LINKS_BASE_URL``.\nMAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH = 512\n\n_FALSE_VALUES = frozenset({\"false\", \"0\", \"no\"})\n_TRUE_VALUES = frozenset({\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\"})\n\n_VALID_LOG_LEVELS = frozenset(\n {\"CRITICAL\", \"ERROR\", \"WARNING\", \"INFO\", \"DEBUG\", \"NOTSET\"}\n)\n\n\ndef _get(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Read a raw environment value.\n\n Returns the value for ``name`` in ``env`` or ``default`` when the variable\n is missing or empty. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = env.get(name)\n if raw is None:\n return default\n raw = raw.strip()\n if not raw:\n return default\n return raw\n\n\ndef _get_int(\n env: Mapping[str, str],\n name: str,\n default: str,\n minimum: int,\n maximum: int,\n) -> int:\n \"\"\"Read an integer setting, clamped to a safe range.\n\n Returns the parsed integer, or the parsed default when the environment\n value is not an integer or falls outside ``[minimum, maximum]``. Raises\n nothing; an unusable value is logged and the default is used so a typo in\n deployment configuration cannot take the service down.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, default)\n try:\n value = int(raw)\n except ValueError:\n logger.warning(\"Invalid integer for %s; using default %s\", name, default)\n value = int(default)\n if value < minimum or value > maximum:\n logger.warning(\n \"Value for %s outside [%d, %d]; using default %s\",\n name,\n minimum,\n maximum,\n default,\n )\n value = int(default)\n return value\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_true(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to true.\n\n Returns ``False`` only for the case-insensitive values ``false``, ``0`` or\n ``no``; every other value (including absence) yields ``True``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"true\").lower()\n return raw not in _FALSE_VALUES\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_false(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to false.\n\n Returns ``True`` only for the case-insensitive values ``true``, ``1`` or\n ``yes``; every other value (including absence) yields ``False``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"false\").lower()\n return raw in _TRUE_VALUES\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Settings:\n \"\"\"Immutable snapshot of the service configuration.\"\"\"\n\n db_path: str\n base_url: str\n default_ttl_days: int\n max_url_length: int\n code_length: int\n code_max_attempts: int\n rate_limit_max: int\n rate_limit_window_seconds: int\n rate_limit_enabled: bool\n trust_forwarded_for: bool\n stats_default_limit: int\n stats_max_limit: int\n dns_resolution_enabled: bool\n log_level: str\n\n @classmethod\n def from_env(cls, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> \"Settings\":\n \"\"\"Build a :class:`Settings` instance from the process environment.\n\n ``env`` defaults to :data:`os.environ`. Returns a fully populated,\n range-checked settings object. Raises nothing: invalid values fall back\n to the documented defaults.\n \"\"\"\n source: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ if env is None else env\n\n db_path = _get(source, \"LINKS_DB_PATH\", \"./links.db\")\n base_url = _get(source, \"LINKS_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:8000\").rstrip(\"/\")\n if len(base_url) > MAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH:\n logger.warning(\"LINKS_BASE_URL too long; using default\")\n base_url = \"http://localhost:8000\"\n\n max_url_length = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH\", \"2048\", 1, ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH\n )\n stats_max_limit = _get_int(source, \"LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT\", \"500\", 1, 10000)\n stats_default_limit = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT\", \"50\", 1, 10000\n )\n if stats_default_limit > stats_max_limit:\n stats_default_limit = stats_max_limit\n\n log_level = _get(source, \"LINKS_LOG_LEVEL\", \"INFO\").upper()\n if log_level not in _VALID_LOG_LEVELS:\n log_level = \"INFO\"\n\n return cls(\n db_path=db_path,\n base_url=base_url,\n default_ttl_days=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS\", \"30\", 1, 36500\n ),\n max_url_length=max_url_length,\n code_length=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_CODE_LENGTH\", \"7\", 4, 32),\n code_max_attempts=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS\", \"5\", 1, 100\n ),\n rate_limit_max=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\", \"10\", 1, 1000000),\n rate_limit_window_seconds=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS\", \"60\", 1, 86400\n ),\n rate_limit_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED\"\n ),\n trust_forwarded_for=_get_bool_default_false(\n source, \"LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR\"\n ),\n stats_default_limit=stats_default_limit,\n stats_max_limit=stats_max_limit,\n dns_resolution_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED\"\n ),\n log_level=log_level,\n )\n\n\n--- app/db.py ---\n\"\"\"SQLite access helpers.\n\nOne connection per request; WAL mode so a click insert does not block\nconcurrent redirect reads. Every statement in the service is parameterized.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.db\")\n\nSCHEMA_STATEMENTS = (\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n url TEXT NOT NULL,\n created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n expires_at TEXT NOT NULL\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clicks (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n link_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES links(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n clicked_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n referrer TEXT,\n user_agent TEXT\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clicks_link_time\n ON clicks(link_id, clicked_at DESC, id DESC)\n \"\"\",\n)\n\n\ndef connect(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n \"\"\"Open a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Returns a connection with\n ``sqlite3.Row`` rows, foreign keys enforced, WAL journalling and a 5 second\n busy timeout. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n try:\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL\")\n except sqlite3.DatabaseError: # pragma: no cover - filesystem dependent\n logger.warning(\"WAL journal mode unavailable; continuing with default\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL\")\n return conn\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef get_connection(db_path: str) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n \"\"\"Context manager yielding a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Yields the connection and always\n closes it. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = connect(db_path)\n try:\n yield conn\n finally:\n conn.close()\n\n\ndef init_db(db_path: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create the schema if it does not exist.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path; its parent directory is created when\n missing. Returns nothing. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` or :class:`OSError`\n when the database or its directory cannot be created.\n \"\"\"\n parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n if parent:\n os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)\n with get_connection(db_path) as conn:\n for statement in SCHEMA_STATEMENTS:\n conn.execute(statement)\n conn.commit()\n\n\n--- app/errors.py ---\n\"\"\"Stable error envelope shared by every endpoint.\n\nAll failures are rendered as ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\nNo stack trace, database message or filesystem path is ever placed in a\nresponse body.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Mapping, Optional\n\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\n\nMAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 400\n\n\nclass ApiError(Exception):\n \"\"\"Application level error carrying an HTTP status and a stable code.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(\n self,\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n ) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create an API error.\n\n ``status_code`` is the HTTP status to return, ``code`` the stable\n machine readable identifier, ``message`` a human readable, non\n sensitive explanation and ``headers`` optional extra response headers.\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n super().__init__(message)\n self.status_code = status_code\n self.code = code\n self.message = message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]\n self.headers: Dict[str, str] = dict(headers or {})\n\n\ndef error_payload(code: str, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Build the canonical error body.\n\n Returns a dict of the shape ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return {\"error\": {\"code\": code, \"message\": message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]}}\n\n\ndef error_response(\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Build a JSON error response using the canonical envelope.\n\n Returns a :class:`fastapi.responses.JSONResponse` with ``status_code`` and\n the given headers. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return JSONResponse(\n status_code=status_code,\n content=error_payload(code, message),\n headers=dict(headers or {}),\n )\n\n\n--- app/main.py ---\n\"\"\"FastAPI application for the URL shortener service.\n\nEndpoints:\n POST /api/links create a short link\n GET /api/links/{code}/stats per-link click analytics\n GET /health liveness probe\n GET /{code} redirect to the stored destination\n\nThe redirect destination always comes from the validated, stored row; it can\nnever come from a query parameter, header or path segment of the request.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\nfrom typing import Any, AsyncIterator, Dict, List, Optional\nfrom urllib.parse import quote\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Query, Request, Response\nfrom fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException\n\nfrom . import codes as codes_module\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .db import get_connection, init_db\nfrom .errors import ApiError, error_response\nfrom .models import CreateLinkRequest, CreateLinkResponse, HealthResponse, StatsResponse\nfrom .ratelimit import RateLimitMiddleware\nfrom .validation import validate_destination_url\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links\")\n\nTIMESTAMP_FORMAT = \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ\"\nMAX_HEADER_STORE_LENGTH = 2048\n_LOCATION_SAFE_CHARACTERS = \":/?#[]@!$&'()*+,;=%~-._\"\n\n\nclass ClientAddressRedactionFilter(logging.Filter):\n \"\"\"Logging filter that removes the client address from access log records.\"\"\"\n\n def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Replace the client address argument of an access log record.\n\n ``record`` is the log record being emitted. Returns ``True`` so the\n record is always kept, after blanking the first positional argument\n which uvicorn uses for the client address. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if isinstance(record.args, tuple) and record.args:\n record.args = (\"-\",) + tuple(record.args[1:])\n return True\n\n\ndef configure_logging(level: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Configure root logging and strip client addresses from access logs.\n\n ``level`` is a logging level name such as ``INFO``. Returns nothing. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n numeric = getattr(logging, level.upper(), logging.INFO)\n logging.basicConfig(\n level=numeric,\n format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s\",\n )\n logging.getLogger().setLevel(numeric)\n access_logger = logging.getLogger(\"uvicorn.access\")\n if not any(\n isinstance(existing, ClientAddressRedactionFilter)\n for existing in access_logger.filters\n ):\n access_logger.addFilter(ClientAddressRedactionFilter())\n\n\ndef format_timestamp(value: datetime) -> str:\n \"\"\"Render an aware datetime in the fixed stored UTC format.\n\n ``value`` is a timezone aware datetime (naive input is treated as UTC).\n Returns a string like ``2024-01-31T12:00:00.000000Z``. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if value.tzinfo is None:\n value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime(TIMESTAMP_FORMAT)\n\n\ndef parse_timestamp(value: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a stored timestamp back into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``value`` is a stored timestamp string. Returns an aware UTC datetime.\n Raises :class:`ValueError` when the string cannot be parsed.\n \"\"\"\n try:\n return datetime.strptime(value, TIMESTAMP_FORMAT).replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n except ValueError:\n pass\n text = value.strip()\n if text.endswith((\"Z\", \"z\")):\n text = text[:-1] + \"+00:00\"\n parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(text)\n if parsed.tzinfo is None:\n parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef parse_client_expiry(raw: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a client supplied ISO-8601 expiry into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``raw`` is the ``expires_at`` value from the request body. Returns the\n instant normalized to UTC. Raises :class:`app.errors.ApiError` with status\n 400 and code ``invalid_expiry`` when the value is not a valid ISO-8601\n timestamp.\n \"\"\"\n text = raw.strip()\n if not text:\n raise ApiError(\n 400, \"invalid_expiry\", \"expires_at must be a valid ISO-8601 timestamp.\"\n )\n candidate = text[:-1] + \"+00:00\" if text.endswith((\"Z\", \"z\")) else text\n try:\n parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(candidate)\n except ValueError:\n raise ApiError(\n 400, \"invalid_expiry\", \"expires_at must be a valid ISO-8601 timestamp.\"\n )\n if parsed.tzinfo is None:\n parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef generate_code(length: int) -> str:\n \"\"\"Generate a CSPRNG short code.\n\n ``length`` is the number of base62 characters. Returns the generated code.\n Raises :class:`ValueError` when ``length`` is not positive.\n \"\"\"\n return codes_module.generate_code(length)\n\n\ndef clip_header(value: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:\n \"\"\"Truncate a header value for storage.\n\n ``value`` is a header value or ``None``. Returns the value truncated to\n 2048 characters, or ``None`` when the header was absent. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if value is None:\n return None\n return value[:MAX_HEADER_STORE_LENGTH]\n\n\ndef location_header_value(url: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Render a stored URL as a Location header value.\n\n ``url`` is the stored destination. Returns the URL byte-for-byte when it is\n ASCII, otherwise a percent-encoded form so the header stays transmittable.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if url.isascii():\n return url\n return quote(url, safe=_LOCATION_SAFE_CHARACTERS)\n\n\ndef create_app(settings: Optional[Settings] = None) -> FastAPI:\n \"\"\"Build the FastAPI application.\n\n ``settings`` overrides the environment derived configuration when given.\n Returns a fully wired :class:`fastapi.FastAPI` instance with the schema\n created. Raises nothing; a failure to create the schema at build time is\n logged and retried at startup and on each request.\n \"\"\"\n resolved = settings if settings is not None else Settings.from_env()\n configure_logging(resolved.log_level)\n\n try:\n init_db(resolved.db_path)\n except (sqlite3.Error, OSError):\n logger.warning(\"Database initialisation deferred to startup\", exc_info=True)\n\n @asynccontextmanager\n async def lifespan(_: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:\n \"\"\"Create the schema at startup.\n\n Yields control to the running application. Raises nothing; database\n problems are logged and surfaced by the health endpoint.\n \"\"\"\n try:\n init_db(resolved.db_path)\n except (sqlite3.Error, OSError):\n logger.error(\"Database initialisation failed at startup\", exc_info=True)\n yield\n\n app = FastAPI(\n title=\"URL Shortener\",\n version=\"1.0.0\",\n description=\"Short links with expiry and IP-free click analytics.\",\n lifespan=lifespan,\n )\n app.state.settings = resolved\n app.state.code_pattern = re.compile(r\"^[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\" % resolved.code_length)\n app.add_middleware(RateLimitMiddleware, settings=resolved)\n\n # ---------------------------------------------------------------- errors\n\n @app.exception_handler(ApiError)\n async def handle_api_error(_: Request, exc: ApiError) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render an :class:`ApiError` using the stable envelope.\n\n Returns the JSON error response with the error's status and headers.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return error_response(exc.status_code, exc.code, exc.message, exc.headers)\n\n @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n async def handle_validation_error(\n _: Request, exc: RequestValidationError\n ) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render a request validation failure as 422 ``validation_error``.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n details: List[str] = []\n for error in exc.errors()[:5]:\n location = \".\".join(\n str(part) for part in error.get(\"loc\", ()) if part != \"body\"\n )\n details.append(f\"{location or 'body'}: {error.get('msg', 'invalid value')}\")\n message = \"Request validation failed.\"\n if details:\n message = f\"{message} {'; '.join(details)}\"\n return error_response(422, \"validation_error\", message)\n\n @app.exception_handler(StarletteHTTPException)\n async def handle_http_exception(\n _: Request, exc: StarletteHTTPException\n ) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render framework HTTP exceptions using the stable envelope.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if exc.status_code == 404:\n code = \"not_found\"\n message = \"The requested resource does not exist.\"\n elif exc.status_code == 405:\n code = \"method_not_allowed\"\n message = \"The HTTP method is not allowed for this resource.\"\n else:\n code = \"http_error\"\n message = \"The request could not be completed.\"\n headers = dict(getattr(exc, \"headers\", None) or {})\n return error_response(exc.status_code, code, message, headers)\n\n @app.exception_handler(sqlite3.Error)\n async def handle_database_error(_: Request, __: sqlite3.Error) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render a database failure as 503 without leaking driver detail.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n logger.error(\"Database error while handling request\", exc_info=True)\n return error_response(\n 503,\n \"service_unavailable\",\n \"The service is temporarily unable to handle the request.\",\n )\n\n @app.exception_handler(Exception)\n async def handle_unexpected_error(_: Request, __: Exception) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render an unexpected failure as a generic 500.\n\n Returns the JSON error response with no internal detail. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n logger.error(\"Unhandled error while handling request\", exc_info=True)\n return error_response(\n 500, \"internal_error\", \"An unexpected error occurred.\"\n )\n\n # ---------------------------------------------------------------- routes\n\n @app.get(\"/health\", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=[\"system\"])\n def health(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Report liveness without reading any user data.\n\n Returns ``{\"status\": \"ok\"}`` with 200 when SQLite answers ``SELECT 1``,\n or ``{\"status\": \"degraded\"}`` with 503 otherwise. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n try:\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n conn.execute(\"SELECT 1\").fetchone()\n except Exception: # noqa: BLE001 - health must never raise\n logger.warning(\"Health check failed\", exc_info=True)\n return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={\"status\": \"degraded\"})\n return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content={\"status\": \"ok\"})\n\n @app.post(\n \"/api/links\",\n status_code=201,\n response_model=CreateLinkResponse,\n tags=[\"links\"],\n )\n def create_link(payload: CreateLinkRequest, request: Request) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Create a short link for a validated public http(s) destination.\n\n ``payload`` is the validated request body. Returns the created link\n representation (code, short_url, url, created_at, expires_at). Raises\n :class:`ApiError` 422 ``validation_error`` for an over-long or blank\n url, 400 ``unsupported_scheme`` / ``invalid_url`` / ``blocked_destination``\n for a rejected destination, 400 ``invalid_expiry`` for a bad expiry, and\n 503 ``code_generation_failed`` when no unique code could be allocated.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n url = payload.url\n if len(url) > current.max_url_length:\n raise ApiError(\n 422,\n \"validation_error\",\n f\"url must be at most {current.max_url_length} characters.\",\n )\n if not url.strip():\n raise ApiError(422, \"validation_error\", \"url must not be empty.\")\n\n validate_destination_url(url, dns_enabled=current.dns_resolution_enabled)\n\n now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n if payload.expires_at is None:\n expires = now + timedelta(days=current.default_ttl_days)\n else:\n expires = parse_client_expiry(payload.expires_at)\n if expires <= now:\n raise ApiError(\n 400, \"invalid_expiry\", \"expires_at must be in the future.\"\n )\n\n created_text = format_timestamp(now)\n expires_text = format_timestamp(expires)\n\n code: Optional[str] = None\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n for _ in range(current.code_max_attempts):\n candidate = generate_code(current.code_length)\n try:\n conn.execute(\n \"INSERT INTO links (code, url, created_at, expires_at) \"\n \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n (candidate, url, created_text, expires_text),\n )\n conn.commit()\n except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n conn.rollback()\n continue\n code = candidate\n break\n\n if code is None:\n raise ApiError(\n 503,\n \"code_generation_failed\",\n \"Could not allocate a unique short code. Please retry.\",\n )\n\n return {\n \"code\": code,\n \"short_url\": f\"{current.base_url}/{code}\",\n \"url\": url,\n \"created_at\": created_text,\n \"expires_at\": expires_text,\n }\n\n @app.get(\n \"/api/links/{code}/stats\",\n response_model=StatsResponse,\n tags=[\"links\"],\n )\n def link_stats(\n code: str,\n request: Request,\n limit: Optional[int] = Query(default=None, description=\"Clicks per page.\"),\n offset: int = Query(default=0, description=\"Clicks to skip.\"),\n ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Return per-link analytics, including for expired links.\n\n ``code`` identifies the link; ``limit`` and ``offset`` page the newest\n first clicks array. Returns the link metadata, the total click count and\n the requested page of clicks. Raises :class:`ApiError` 404 ``not_found``\n for an unknown code and 422 ``validation_error`` for out of range\n pagination parameters.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n pattern: re.Pattern[str] = request.app.state.code_pattern\n if not pattern.match(code):\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n effective_limit = current.stats_default_limit if limit is None else limit\n if effective_limit < 1 or effective_limit > current.stats_max_limit:\n raise ApiError(\n 422,\n \"validation_error\",\n f\"limit must be between 1 and {current.stats_max_limit}.\",\n )\n if offset < 0:\n raise ApiError(422, \"validation_error\", \"offset must be zero or greater.\")\n\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n link_row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT id, code, url, created_at, expires_at FROM links \"\n \"WHERE code = ?\",\n (code,),\n ).fetchone()\n if link_row is None:\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n total_row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM clicks WHERE link_id = ?\",\n (link_row[\"id\"],),\n ).fetchone()\n click_rows = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT clicked_at, referrer, user_agent FROM clicks \"\n \"WHERE link_id = ? ORDER BY clicked_at DESC, id DESC \"\n \"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?\",\n (link_row[\"id\"], effective_limit, offset),\n ).fetchall()\n\n clicks = [\n {\n \"timestamp\": row[\"clicked_at\"],\n \"referrer\": row[\"referrer\"],\n \"user_agent\": row[\"user_agent\"],\n }\n for row in click_rows\n ]\n return {\n \"code\": link_row[\"code\"],\n \"url\": link_row[\"url\"],\n \"created_at\": link_row[\"created_at\"],\n \"expires_at\": link_row[\"expires_at\"],\n \"total_clicks\": int(total_row[\"total\"]) if total_row else 0,\n \"clicks\": clicks,\n }\n\n @app.get(\"/{code}\", include_in_schema=False)\n def redirect_to_target(code: str, request: Request) -> Response:\n \"\"\"Redirect a visitor to the stored destination and record the click.\n\n ``code`` is the short code from the path; the destination is read from\n the stored, previously validated row and never from request input.\n Returns a 307 response whose Location is the stored URL, with\n ``Cache-Control: no-store``. Raises :class:`ApiError` 404 ``not_found``\n for unknown or malformed codes and 410 ``link_expired`` for lapsed\n links; neither writes a click row.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n pattern: re.Pattern[str] = request.app.state.code_pattern\n if not pattern.match(code):\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT id, url, expires_at FROM links WHERE code = ?\",\n (code,),\n ).fetchone()\n if row is None:\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n try:\n expires = parse_timestamp(row[\"expires_at\"])\n except ValueError:\n # Fail closed: an unreadable expiry is treated as expired.\n logger.error(\"Unparsable expires_at for a stored link\")\n raise ApiError(410, \"link_expired\", \"This link has expired.\")\n\n if now >= expires:\n raise ApiError(410, \"link_expired\", \"This link has expired.\")\n\n conn.execute(\n \"INSERT INTO clicks (link_id, clicked_at, referrer, user_agent) \"\n \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n (\n row[\"id\"],\n format_timestamp(now),\n clip_header(request.headers.get(\"referer\")),\n clip_header(request.headers.get(\"user-agent\")),\n ),\n )\n conn.commit()\n destination = row[\"url\"]\n\n return Response(\n status_code=307,\n headers={\n \"Location\": location_header_value(destination),\n \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\",\n \"Referrer-Policy\": \"no-referrer\",\n },\n )\n\n return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\": # pragma: no cover - Keys are a per-boot salted SHA-256 of the client\naddress: the address itself is never stored, persisted or logged, and all state\nvanishes on restart.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport math\nimport re\nimport secrets\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom collections import OrderedDict, deque\nfrom typing import Deque, Optional, Tuple\n\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .errors import error_response\n\n#: Redirects are far cheaper and far more frequent than creations, so the\n#: redirect bucket uses a multiple of the configured creation budget. The\n#: design pins only the creation limit; this keeps redirects protected without\n#: introducing a new environment variable.\nREDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER = 100\n\n#: Upper bound on tracked keys so the limiter cannot grow without limit.\nMAX_TRACKED_KEYS = 20000\n\nCREATE_PATH = \"/api/links\"\n\n\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\n \"\"\"Rate limit link creation and redirects in a single place.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings) -> None:\n \"\"\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\n\n ``app`` is the downstream ASGI application and ``settings`` the service\n configuration. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self.app = app\n self.settings = settings\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(32)\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\n self._hits: \"OrderedDict[str, Deque[float]]\" = OrderedDict()\n self._code_pattern = re.compile(\n r\"^/[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\" % settings.code_length\n )\n\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n \"\"\"ASGI entry point.\n\n ``scope``, ``receive`` and ``send`` are the standard ASGI arguments.\n Returns nothing; either forwards the call downstream or emits a 429 with\n a ``Retry-After`` header. Raises nothing of its own.\n \"\"\"\n if scope.get(\"type\") != \"http\" or not self.settings.rate_limit_enabled:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n rule = self._rule_for(str(scope.get(\"method\", \"\")), str(scope.get(\"path\", \"\")))\n if rule is None:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n bucket, limit = rule\n allowed, retry_after = self._consume(bucket, self._client_key(scope, bucket), limit)\n if allowed:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n response = error_response(\n 429,\n \"rate_limited\",\n \"Too many requests. Please retry later.\",\n headers={\"Retry-After\": str(retry_after), \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\"},\n )\n await response(scope, receive, send)\n\n def _rule_for(self, method: str, path: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, int]]:\n \"\"\"Decide which limiter bucket applies to a request.\n\n ``method`` is the HTTP method and ``path`` the request path. Returns a\n ``(bucket, limit)`` pair for rate limited routes, or ``None`` when the\n route is not limited. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n normalized = path.rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n if method.upper() == \"POST\" and normalized == CREATE_PATH:\n return \"create\", self.settings.rate_limit_max\n if method.upper() in {\"GET\", \"HEAD\"} and self._code_pattern.match(path):\n return \"redirect\", self.settings.rate_limit_max * REDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER\n return None\n\n def _client_address(self, scope: Scope) -> str:\n \"\"\"Determine the client address used for keying.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope. Returns the first ``X-Forwarded-For`` entry\n when the service is configured to trust it, otherwise the peer address,\n or ``\"unknown\"``. The value is only hashed, never stored or logged.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if self.settings.trust_forwarded_for:\n for name, value in scope.get(\"headers\") or []:\n if name == b\"x-forwarded-for\":\n first = value.decode(\"latin-1\", \"ignore\").split(\",\")[0].strip()\n if first:\n return first[:128]\n break\n client = scope.get(\"client\")\n if client:\n try:\n return str(client[0])[:128]\n except (IndexError, TypeError): # pragma: no cover - defensive\n return \"unknown\"\n return \"unknown\"\n\n def _client_key(self, scope: Scope, bucket: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Build the salted hash key for a client and bucket.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope and ``bucket`` the limiter bucket name.\n Returns a hex SHA-256 digest that cannot be reversed to an IP address.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n digest = hashlib.sha256()\n digest.update(self._salt)\n digest.update(bucket.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n digest.update(b\"\\x00\")\n digest.update(self._client_address(scope).encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n return digest.hexdigest()\n\n def _consume(self, bucket: str, key: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n \"\"\"Record a request against the rolling window.\n\n ``bucket`` is the limiter bucket (used only for readability), ``key``\n the hashed client key and ``limit`` the allowed number of requests per\n window. Returns ``(allowed, retry_after_seconds)`` where\n ``retry_after_seconds`` is at least 1 when the request is denied.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n now = time.monotonic()\n window = float(self.settings.rate_limit_window_seconds)\n with self._lock:\n hits = self._hits.get(key)\n if hits is None:\n hits = deque()\n self._hits[key] = hits\n self._hits.move_to_end(key)\n while hits and (now - hits[0]) >= window:\n hits.popleft()\n if len(hits) >= limit:\n retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(window - (now - hits[0])))\n return False, retry_after\n hits.append(now)\n self._prune_locked()\n return True, 0\n\n def _prune_locked(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Drop stale limiter state while the lock is held.\n\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if len(self._hits) <= MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n return\n for stale_key in [k for k, v in self._hits.items() if not v]:\n self._hits.pop(stale_key, None)\n while len(self._hits) > MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n self._hits.popitem(last=False)\n\n\n--- app/validation.py ---\n\"\"\"Destination URL validation.\n\nOnly ``http`` and ``https`` URLs are accepted (allow-list, not a block-list of\nbad schemes). Hosts are resolved and every resulting address is checked against\nthe denied ranges: loopback, private, link-local (including the cloud metadata\naddress 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and\nother reserved space. No HTTP request is ever made to a user supplied URL.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport ipaddress\nimport logging\nimport socket\nfrom typing import List, Optional, Tuple, Union\nfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n\nfrom .errors import ApiError\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.validation\")\n\nIPAddress = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Address, ipaddress.IPv6Address]\nIPNetwork = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Network, ipaddress.IPv6Network]\n\nALLOWED_SCHEMES = frozenset({\"http\", \"https\"})\nMAX_HOST_LENGTH = 253\n\nBLOCKED_MESSAGE = (\n \"The destination host is not a permitted public address.\"\n)\n\n#: Explicitly denied singleton addresses. 169.254.169.254 is the cloud\n#: metadata endpoint; Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if not token:\n return None\n lowered = token.lower()\n try:\n if lowered.startswith(\"0x\"):\n digits = lowered[2:]\n if not digits or any(c not in \"0123456789abcdef\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 16)\n elif len(lowered) > 1 and lowered.startswith(\"0\"):\n digits = lowered[1:]\n if any(c not in \"01234567\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 8)\n else:\n if not lowered.isdigit():\n return None\n value = int(lowered, 10)\n except ValueError:\n return None\n if value < 0 or value > 0xFFFFFFFF:\n return None\n return value\n\n\ndef parse_relaxed_ipv4(host: str) -> Optional[ipaddress.IPv4Address]:\n \"\"\"Decode non canonical IPv4 literals (decimal, octal, hex, short forms).\n\n ``host`` is the host component of a URL. Returns the decoded\n :class:`ipaddress.IPv4Address` when the host is any ``inet_aton`` style\n literal such as ``2130706433``, ``0x7f.0.0.1`` or ``127.1``; returns\n ``None`` when the host is not such a literal. PRIOR ANSWERS are decisions a human already made about this requirement; treat them as authoritative, do not raise them again, and let them raise your confidence.\n\nNormalize the requirement into the schema.", "skill_id": "analyze", "system": "You are the requirement analyst for an automated software delivery pipeline. Design, code, tests and release checks are all generated from your output and no human reads the requirement again before code exists. An assumption you make quietly becomes a defect that nobody traces back to this step.\n\nYour job is to turn one natural language requirement into an intent, testable acceptance criteria, hard constraints, and an honest account of what the requirement does not say.\n\nYou have two ways to handle a gap, and choosing between them is most of this job.\n\nThe first is a documented assumption. Pick the defensible industry default, record it in notes as \"Assumed: X, because Y\", and set blocking false. This is right whenever a competent engineer would simply decide and move on, and whenever the decision is cheap to revisit. Most gaps are this. Short code length, default page size, whether analytics store a counter or rows, which of 302 and 307 to use: these have defaults, so take them.\n\nThe second is a blocking question, blocking true. Reserve it for gaps with no defensible default, or where guessing wrong is expensive and hard to undo. Three things usually qualify: the security or privacy posture, compatibility with something that already exists, and scope that changes what is being built rather than how. Everything else is an assumption.\n\nCalibrate against this test. If a senior engineer could start work today on what you were given, you do not have a blocking ambiguity, you have assumptions to write down. Blocking on a requirement that is merely incomplete is its own failure, symmetrical with guessing and just as costly, because it stops delivery to ask questions nobody needed answered. More than about three blocking questions on a requirement a person could start on means you are over blocking; re-read them and convert the ones that have obvious defaults.\n\nAcceptance criteria are observable. \"Handles errors gracefully\" is not a criterion. \"A create request whose target uses a non http scheme returns 400 and writes no row\" is. Each one should map to a single test.\n\nConstraints are limits the solution must respect: runtime, framework, storage, latency budget, compatibility, security requirement. Do not smuggle design choices in as constraints; if you are choosing rather than obeying, it belongs to the design stage.\n\nName the security requirements the requirement implies but never states. A service that stores and follows user supplied URLs implies open redirect and server side request forgery handling whether or not anyone wrote that down. Record those as constraints when they are not negotiable, as ambiguities only when the strictness is genuinely a product decision.\n\nconfidence is your probability that a competent engineer, given this normalization plus your stated assumptions, ships something the requester would accept. It measures whether work can start, not whether the requirement is complete. Documented assumptions do not lower it; unresolved blocking ambiguities do, and any of those puts it below 0.6. Do not inflate it and do not deflate it. A low score routes the task to a human, which is the system working. A low score on a requirement that was actually workable is the system failing quietly.\n\nscenario is greenfield when there is no code to respect, brownfield when existing code is supplied and must keep working, ambiguous when you cannot tell from what you were given. The variable SHORTENER_API_KEYS holds a comma-separated list of key:quota pairs, so a key named alpha with a quota of 100 and a key named beta with a quota of 20 are written as two such pairs. The quota is requests per minute for link creation. There is no admin surface and no schema change; an unknown key is treated as no key. A caller presents its key in an X-API-Key header. A request carrying a known key is limited against that key's quota. A request with no key, or an unknown one, keeps the existing per-IP limit exactly as it behaves today, so existing clients continue to work unchanged. A caller over its quota receives HTTP 429 with a Retry-After header giving whole seconds until the window resets. This change is additive. The existing per-IP throttle on redirects stays exactly as it is; do not remove or weaken it. Per-key limiting applies only to link creation, and redirects are never limited by key.", "scenario": "brownfield"}, "prompt": "REQUIREMENT\nAdd per-API-key rate limiting to the existing URL shortener.\n\nKeys come from the environment, not from the database. The variable SHORTENER_API_KEYS holds a comma-separated list of key:quota pairs, so a key named alpha with a quota of 100 and a key named beta with a quota of 20 are written as two such pairs. The quota is requests per minute for link creation. There is no admin surface and no schema change; an unknown key is treated as no key.\n\nA caller presents its key in an X-API-Key header. A request carrying a known key is limited against that key's quota. A request with no key, or an unknown one, keeps the existing per-IP limit exactly as it behaves today, so existing clients continue to work unchanged.\n\nA caller over its quota receives HTTP 429 with a Retry-After header giving whole seconds until the window resets.\n\nThis change is additive. The existing per-IP throttle on redirects stays exactly as it is; do not remove or weaken it. Per-key limiting applies only to link creation, and redirects are never limited by key.\n\nEXISTING CODE\n--- README.md ---\n# URL Shortener\n\nPublic JSON API that turns a caller supplied public `http(s)` URL into an\nunguessable base62 short code, redirects visitors until the link expires and\nexposes per-link click analytics that contain no visitor IP addresses.\n\n## Run\n\n```bash\npip install -r requirements.txt\nuvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000\n```\n\n## Endpoints\n\n| Method | Path | Purpose |\n| ------ | ---- | ------- |\n| POST | `/api/links` | Create a short link (`{\"url\": \"...\", \"expires_at\": \"...\"}`) |\n| GET | `/api/links/{code}/stats` | Click analytics (`limit`, `offset`) |\n| GET | `/health` | Liveness; `{\"status\":\"ok\"}` or 503 `{\"status\":\"degraded\"}` |\n| GET | `/{code}` | 307 redirect to the stored destination |\n\nErrors always use `{\"error\": {\"code\": \"...\", \"message\": \"...\"}}`.\n\n## Configuration\n\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n| -------- | ------- | ------- |\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite database file |\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` |\n| `LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS` | `30` | Default link lifetime |\n| `LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH` | `2048` | Maximum accepted URL length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert attempts on code collision |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Creations per client per window |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Rolling window length |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | `false`/`0`/`no` disables the limiter |\n| `LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR` | `false` | Use first `X-Forwarded-For` entry |\n| `LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT` | `50` | Default clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT` | `500` | Maximum clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED` | `true` | Resolve and denylist-check hosts |\n| `LINKS_LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\n\nNo secrets are read or stored by the service.\n\n## Security notes\n\n* Scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`) plus an address denylist covering\n loopback, private, link-local (including `169.254.169.254`), unique-local,\n CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and reserved ranges, with IPv4-mapped,\n IPv4-compatible, 6to4, Teredo and decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals decoded.\n* The service never performs an outbound HTTP request to a destination.\n* Redirect targets come only from the validated, stored row.\n* Rate limiting keys on a per-boot salted hash of the client address; no IP is\n written to the database, to a file or to a log.\n\n\n--- app/__init__.py ---\n\"\"\"URL shortener service package.\n\nExposes :func:`app.main.create_app`, the application factory, and the module\nlevel ``app`` ASGI application used by uvicorn.\n\"\"\"\n\n__all__ = [\"__version__\"]\n\n__version__ = \"1.0.0\"\n\n\n--- app/codes.py ---\n\"\"\"Short code generation.\n\nCodes are drawn from a cryptographically secure random source; they are never\nderived from a database row id or any other sequence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport string\n\nALPHABET = string.ascii_uppercase + string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits\n\n\ndef generate_code(length: int) -> str:\n \"\"\"Generate a base62 short code.\n\n ``length`` is the number of characters to produce. Returns a string of\n exactly ``length`` characters drawn uniformly from ``[A-Za-z0-9]`` using\n :mod:`secrets`. Raises :class:`ValueError` when ``length`` is not positive.\n \"\"\"\n if length <= 0:\n raise ValueError(\"code length must be positive\")\n return \"\".join(secrets.choice(ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\n--- app/config.py ---\n\"\"\"Environment driven configuration for the URL shortener service.\n\nAll settings come from environment variables with the exact names and defaults\nfixed by the design document. No secrets are read or stored here.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Mapping, Optional\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.config\")\n\n#: Hard ceiling applied to the accepted ``url`` length regardless of\n#: ``LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH``. It bounds the string that ever reaches the\n#: database and the pydantic request model.\nABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH = 8192\n\n#: Hard ceiling for the client supplied ``expires_at`` string.\nMAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH = 64\n\n#: Hard ceiling for ``LINKS_BASE_URL``.\nMAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH = 512\n\n_FALSE_VALUES = frozenset({\"false\", \"0\", \"no\"})\n_TRUE_VALUES = frozenset({\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\"})\n\n_VALID_LOG_LEVELS = frozenset(\n {\"CRITICAL\", \"ERROR\", \"WARNING\", \"INFO\", \"DEBUG\", \"NOTSET\"}\n)\n\n\ndef _get(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Read a raw environment value.\n\n Returns the value for ``name`` in ``env`` or ``default`` when the variable\n is missing or empty. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = env.get(name)\n if raw is None:\n return default\n raw = raw.strip()\n if not raw:\n return default\n return raw\n\n\ndef _get_int(\n env: Mapping[str, str],\n name: str,\n default: str,\n minimum: int,\n maximum: int,\n) -> int:\n \"\"\"Read an integer setting, clamped to a safe range.\n\n Returns the parsed integer, or the parsed default when the environment\n value is not an integer or falls outside ``[minimum, maximum]``. Raises\n nothing; an unusable value is logged and the default is used so a typo in\n deployment configuration cannot take the service down.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, default)\n try:\n value = int(raw)\n except ValueError:\n logger.warning(\"Invalid integer for %s; using default %s\", name, default)\n value = int(default)\n if value < minimum or value > maximum:\n logger.warning(\n \"Value for %s outside [%d, %d]; using default %s\",\n name,\n minimum,\n maximum,\n default,\n )\n value = int(default)\n return value\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_true(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to true.\n\n Returns ``False`` only for the case-insensitive values ``false``, ``0`` or\n ``no``; every other value (including absence) yields ``True``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"true\").lower()\n return raw not in _FALSE_VALUES\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_false(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to false.\n\n Returns ``True`` only for the case-insensitive values ``true``, ``1`` or\n ``yes``; every other value (including absence) yields ``False``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"false\").lower()\n return raw in _TRUE_VALUES\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Settings:\n \"\"\"Immutable snapshot of the service configuration.\"\"\"\n\n db_path: str\n base_url: str\n default_ttl_days: int\n max_url_length: int\n code_length: int\n code_max_attempts: int\n rate_limit_max: int\n rate_limit_window_seconds: int\n rate_limit_enabled: bool\n trust_forwarded_for: bool\n stats_default_limit: int\n stats_max_limit: int\n dns_resolution_enabled: bool\n log_level: str\n\n @classmethod\n def from_env(cls, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> \"Settings\":\n \"\"\"Build a :class:`Settings` instance from the process environment.\n\n ``env`` defaults to :data:`os.environ`. Returns a fully populated,\n range-checked settings object. Raises nothing: invalid values fall back\n to the documented defaults.\n \"\"\"\n source: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ if env is None else env\n\n db_path = _get(source, \"LINKS_DB_PATH\", \"./links.db\")\n base_url = _get(source, \"LINKS_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:8000\").rstrip(\"/\")\n if len(base_url) > MAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH:\n logger.warning(\"LINKS_BASE_URL too long; using default\")\n base_url = \"http://localhost:8000\"\n\n max_url_length = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH\", \"2048\", 1, ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH\n )\n stats_max_limit = _get_int(source, \"LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT\", \"500\", 1, 10000)\n stats_default_limit = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT\", \"50\", 1, 10000\n )\n if stats_default_limit > stats_max_limit:\n stats_default_limit = stats_max_limit\n\n log_level = _get(source, \"LINKS_LOG_LEVEL\", \"INFO\").upper()\n if log_level not in _VALID_LOG_LEVELS:\n log_level = \"INFO\"\n\n return cls(\n db_path=db_path,\n base_url=base_url,\n default_ttl_days=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS\", \"30\", 1, 36500\n ),\n max_url_length=max_url_length,\n code_length=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_CODE_LENGTH\", \"7\", 4, 32),\n code_max_attempts=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS\", \"5\", 1, 100\n ),\n rate_limit_max=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\", \"10\", 1, 1000000),\n rate_limit_window_seconds=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS\", \"60\", 1, 86400\n ),\n rate_limit_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED\"\n ),\n trust_forwarded_for=_get_bool_default_false(\n source, \"LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR\"\n ),\n stats_default_limit=stats_default_limit,\n stats_max_limit=stats_max_limit,\n dns_resolution_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED\"\n ),\n log_level=log_level,\n )\n\n\n--- app/db.py ---\n\"\"\"SQLite access helpers.\n\nOne connection per request; WAL mode so a click insert does not block\nconcurrent redirect reads. Every statement in the service is parameterized.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.db\")\n\nSCHEMA_STATEMENTS = (\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n url TEXT NOT NULL,\n created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n expires_at TEXT NOT NULL\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clicks (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n link_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES links(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n clicked_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n referrer TEXT,\n user_agent TEXT\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clicks_link_time\n ON clicks(link_id, clicked_at DESC, id DESC)\n \"\"\",\n)\n\n\ndef connect(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n \"\"\"Open a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Returns a connection with\n ``sqlite3.Row`` rows, foreign keys enforced, WAL journalling and a 5 second\n busy timeout. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n try:\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL\")\n except sqlite3.DatabaseError: # pragma: no cover - filesystem dependent\n logger.warning(\"WAL journal mode unavailable; continuing with default\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL\")\n return conn\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef get_connection(db_path: str) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n \"\"\"Context manager yielding a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Yields the connection and always\n closes it. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = connect(db_path)\n try:\n yield conn\n finally:\n conn.close()\n\n\ndef init_db(db_path: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create the schema if it does not exist.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path; its parent directory is created when\n missing. Returns nothing. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` or :class:`OSError`\n when the database or its directory cannot be created.\n \"\"\"\n parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n if parent:\n os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)\n with get_connection(db_path) as conn:\n for statement in SCHEMA_STATEMENTS:\n conn.execute(statement)\n conn.commit()\n\n\n--- app/errors.py ---\n\"\"\"Stable error envelope shared by every endpoint.\n\nAll failures are rendered as ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\nNo stack trace, database message or filesystem path is ever placed in a\nresponse body.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Mapping, Optional\n\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\n\nMAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 400\n\n\nclass ApiError(Exception):\n \"\"\"Application level error carrying an HTTP status and a stable code.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(\n self,\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n ) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create an API error.\n\n ``status_code`` is the HTTP status to return, ``code`` the stable\n machine readable identifier, ``message`` a human readable, non\n sensitive explanation and ``headers`` optional extra response headers.\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n super().__init__(message)\n self.status_code = status_code\n self.code = code\n self.message = message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]\n self.headers: Dict[str, str] = dict(headers or {})\n\n\ndef error_payload(code: str, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Build the canonical error body.\n\n Returns a dict of the shape ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return {\"error\": {\"code\": code, \"message\": message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]}}\n\n\ndef error_response(\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Build a JSON error response using the canonical envelope.\n\n Returns a :class:`fastapi.responses.JSONResponse` with ``status_code`` and\n the given headers. Returns ``True`` so the\n record is always kept, after blanking the first positional argument\n which uvicorn uses for the client address. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if isinstance(record.args, tuple) and record.args:\n record.args = (\"-\",) + tuple(record.args[1:])\n return True\n\n\ndef configure_logging(level: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Configure root logging and strip client addresses from access logs.\n\n ``level`` is a logging level name such as ``INFO``. Returns nothing. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n numeric = getattr(logging, level.upper(), logging.INFO)\n logging.basicConfig(\n level=numeric,\n format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s\",\n )\n logging.getLogger().setLevel(numeric)\n access_logger = logging.getLogger(\"uvicorn.access\")\n if not any(\n isinstance(existing, ClientAddressRedactionFilter)\n for existing in access_logger.filters\n ):\n access_logger.addFilter(ClientAddressRedactionFilter())\n\n\ndef format_timestamp(value: datetime) -> str:\n \"\"\"Render an aware datetime in the fixed stored UTC format.\n\n ``value`` is a timezone aware datetime (naive input is treated as UTC).\n Returns a string like ``2024-01-31T12:00:00.000000Z``. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if value.tzinfo is None:\n value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime(TIMESTAMP_FORMAT)\n\n\ndef parse_timestamp(value: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a stored timestamp back into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``value`` is a stored timestamp string. Returns an aware UTC datetime.\n Raises :class:`ValueError` when the string cannot be parsed.\n \"\"\"\n try:\n return datetime.strptime(value, TIMESTAMP_FORMAT).replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n except ValueError:\n pass\n text = value.strip()\n if text.endswith((\"Z\", \"z\")):\n text = text[:-1] + \"+00:00\"\n parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(text)\n if parsed.tzinfo is None:\n parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef parse_client_expiry(raw: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a client supplied ISO-8601 expiry into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``raw`` is the ``expires_at`` value from the request body. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n details: List[str] = []\n for error in exc.errors()[:5]:\n location = \".\".join(\n str(part) for part in error.get(\"loc\", ()) if part != \"body\"\n )\n details.append(f\"{location or 'body'}: {error.get('msg', 'invalid value')}\")\n message = \"Request validation failed.\"\n if details:\n message = f\"{message} {'; '.join(details)}\"\n return error_response(422, \"validation_error\", message)\n\n @app.exception_handler(StarletteHTTPException)\n async def handle_http_exception(\n _: Request, exc: StarletteHTTPException\n ) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render framework HTTP exceptions using the stable envelope.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if exc.status_code == 404:\n code = \"not_found\"\n message = \"The requested resource does not exist.\"\n elif exc.status_code == 405:\n code = \"method_not_allowed\"\n message = \"The HTTP method is not allowed for this resource.\"\n else:\n code = \"http_error\"\n message = \"The request could not be completed.\"\n headers = dict(getattr(exc, \"headers\", None) or {})\n return error_response(exc.status_code, code, message, headers)\n\n @app.exception_handler(sqlite3.Error)\n async def handle_database_error(_: Request, __: sqlite3.Error) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render a database failure as 503 without leaking driver detail.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Keys are a per-boot salted SHA-256 of the client\naddress: the address itself is never stored, persisted or logged, and all state\nvanishes on restart.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport math\nimport re\nimport secrets\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom collections import OrderedDict, deque\nfrom typing import Deque, Optional, Tuple\n\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .errors import error_response\n\n#: Redirects are far cheaper and far more frequent than creations, so the\n#: redirect bucket uses a multiple of the configured creation budget. The\n#: design pins only the creation limit; this keeps redirects protected without\n#: introducing a new environment variable.\nREDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER = 100\n\n#: Upper bound on tracked keys so the limiter cannot grow without limit.\nMAX_TRACKED_KEYS = 20000\n\nCREATE_PATH = \"/api/links\"\n\n\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\n \"\"\"Rate limit link creation and redirects in a single place.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings) -> None:\n \"\"\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\n\n ``app`` is the downstream ASGI application and ``settings`` the service\n configuration. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self.app = app\n self.settings = settings\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(32)\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\n self._hits: \"OrderedDict[str, Deque[float]]\" = OrderedDict()\n self._code_pattern = re.compile(\n r\"^/[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\" % settings.code_length\n )\n\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n \"\"\"ASGI entry point.\n\n ``scope``, ``receive`` and ``send`` are the standard ASGI arguments.\n Returns nothing; either forwards the call downstream or emits a 429 with\n a ``Retry-After`` header. Raises nothing of its own.\n \"\"\"\n if scope.get(\"type\") != \"http\" or not self.settings.rate_limit_enabled:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n rule = self._rule_for(str(scope.get(\"method\", \"\")), str(scope.get(\"path\", \"\")))\n if rule is None:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n bucket, limit = rule\n allowed, retry_after = self._consume(bucket, self._client_key(scope, bucket), limit)\n if allowed:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n response = error_response(\n 429,\n \"rate_limited\",\n \"Too many requests. Please retry later.\",\n headers={\"Retry-After\": str(retry_after), \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\"},\n )\n await response(scope, receive, send)\n\n def _rule_for(self, method: str, path: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, int]]:\n \"\"\"Decide which limiter bucket applies to a request.\n\n ``method`` is the HTTP method and ``path`` the request path. Returns a\n ``(bucket, limit)`` pair for rate limited routes, or ``None`` when the\n route is not limited. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n normalized = path.rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n if method.upper() == \"POST\" and normalized == CREATE_PATH:\n return \"create\", self.settings.rate_limit_max\n if method.upper() in {\"GET\", \"HEAD\"} and self._code_pattern.match(path):\n return \"redirect\", self.settings.rate_limit_max * REDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER\n return None\n\n def _client_address(self, scope: Scope) -> str:\n \"\"\"Determine the client address used for keying.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope. Returns the first ``X-Forwarded-For`` entry\n when the service is configured to trust it, otherwise the peer address,\n or ``\"unknown\"``. The value is only hashed, never stored or logged.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if self.settings.trust_forwarded_for:\n for name, value in scope.get(\"headers\") or []:\n if name == b\"x-forwarded-for\":\n first = value.decode(\"latin-1\", \"ignore\").split(\",\")[0].strip()\n if first:\n return first[:128]\n break\n client = scope.get(\"client\")\n if client:\n try:\n return str(client[0])[:128]\n except (IndexError, TypeError): # pragma: no cover - defensive\n return \"unknown\"\n return \"unknown\"\n\n def _client_key(self, scope: Scope, bucket: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Build the salted hash key for a client and bucket.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope and ``bucket`` the limiter bucket name.\n Returns a hex SHA-256 digest that cannot be reversed to an IP address.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n digest = hashlib.sha256()\n digest.update(self._salt)\n digest.update(bucket.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n digest.update(b\"\\x00\")\n digest.update(self._client_address(scope).encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n return digest.hexdigest()\n\n def _consume(self, bucket: str, key: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n \"\"\"Record a request against the rolling window.\n\n ``bucket`` is the limiter bucket (used only for readability), ``key``\n the hashed client key and ``limit`` the allowed number of requests per\n window. Returns ``(allowed, retry_after_seconds)`` where\n ``retry_after_seconds`` is at least 1 when the request is denied.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n now = time.monotonic()\n window = float(self.settings.rate_limit_window_seconds)\n with self._lock:\n hits = self._hits.get(key)\n if hits is None:\n hits = deque()\n self._hits[key] = hits\n self._hits.move_to_end(key)\n while hits and (now - hits[0]) >= window:\n hits.popleft()\n if len(hits) >= limit:\n retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(window - (now - hits[0])))\n return False, retry_after\n hits.append(now)\n self._prune_locked()\n return True, 0\n\n def _prune_locked(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Drop stale limiter state while the lock is held.\n\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if len(self._hits) <= MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n return\n for stale_key in [k for k, v in self._hits.items() if not v]:\n self._hits.pop(stale_key, None)\n while len(self._hits) > MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n self._hits.popitem(last=False)\n\n\n--- app/validation.py ---\n\"\"\"Destination URL validation.\n\nOnly ``http`` and ``https`` URLs are accepted (allow-list, not a block-list of\nbad schemes). Hosts are resolved and every resulting address is checked against\nthe denied ranges: loopback, private, link-local (including the cloud metadata\naddress 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and\nother reserved space. No HTTP request is ever made to a user supplied URL.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport ipaddress\nimport logging\nimport socket\nfrom typing import List, Optional, Tuple, Union\nfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n\nfrom .errors import ApiError\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.validation\")\n\nIPAddress = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Address, ipaddress.IPv6Address]\nIPNetwork = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Network, ipaddress.IPv6Network]\n\nALLOWED_SCHEMES = frozenset({\"http\", \"https\"})\nMAX_HOST_LENGTH = 253\n\nBLOCKED_MESSAGE = (\n \"The destination host is not a permitted public address.\"\n)\n\n#: Explicitly denied singleton addresses. 169.254.169.254 is the cloud\n#: metadata endpoint; reaching it turns a URL fetcher into a credential leak.\nCLOUD_METADATA_ADDRESSES = frozenset(\n {\n ipaddress.ip_address(\"169.254.169.254\"),\n ipaddress.ip_address(\"fd00:ec2::254\"),\n }\n)\n\nBLOCKED_NETWORKS: Tuple[IPNetwork, ...] = (\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"0.0.0.0/8\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"10.0.0.0/8\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"100.64.0.0/10\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"127.0.0.0/8\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"169.254.0.0/16\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"172.16.0.0/12\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"192.0.0.0/24\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"192.0.2.0/24\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"192.168.0.0/16\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"198.18.0.0/15\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"198.51.100.0/24\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"203.0.113.0/24\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"224.0.0.0/4\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"240.0.0.0/4\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"255.255.255.255/32\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"::/128\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"::1/128\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"64:ff9b:1::/48\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"100::/64\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"2001:db8::/32\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"fc00::/7\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"fe80::/10\"),\n ipaddress.ip_network(\"ff00::/8\"),\n)\n\n\ndef _parse_int_token(token: str) -> Optional[int]:\n \"\"\"Parse one dotted-quad component allowing decimal, octal and hex forms.\n\n ``token`` is a single component such as ``0x7f``, ``0177`` or ``127``.\n Returns the integer value, or ``None`` when the token is not a valid\n numeric component. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if not token:\n return None\n lowered = token.lower()\n try:\n if lowered.startswith(\"0x\"):\n digits = lowered[2:]\n if not digits or any(c not in \"0123456789abcdef\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 16)\n elif len(lowered) > 1 and lowered.startswith(\"0\"):\n digits = lowered[1:]\n if any(c not in \"01234567\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 8)\n else:\n if not lowered.isdigit():\n return None\n value = int(lowered, 10)\n except ValueError:\n return None\n if value < 0 or value > 0xFFFFFFFF:\n return None\n return value\n\n\ndef parse_relaxed_ipv4(host: str) -> Optional[ipaddress.IPv4Address]:\n \"\"\"Decode non canonical IPv4 literals (decimal, octal, hex, short forms).\n\n ``host`` is the host component of a URL. Returns the decoded\n :class:`ipaddress.IPv4Address` when the host is any ``inet_aton`` style\n literal such as ``2130706433``, ``0x7f.0.0.1`` or ``127.1``; returns\n ``None`` when the host is not such a literal. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n candidate = host.rstrip(\".\")\n if not candidate:\n return None\n parts = candidate.split(\".\")\n if len(parts) > 4:\n return None\n values: List[int] = []\n for part in parts:\n parsed = _parse_int_token(part)\n if parsed is None:\n return None\n values.append(parsed)\n for value in values[:-1]:\n if value > 0xFF:\n return None\n remaining_bytes = 4 - (len(values) - 1)\n last = values[-1]\n if last > (256 ** remaining_bytes) - 1:\n return None\n total = 0\n for index, value in enumerate(values[:-1]):\n total |= value << (8 * (3 - index))\n total |= last\n try:\n return ipaddress.IPv4Address(total)\n except (ipaddress.AddressValueError, ValueError):\n return None\n\n\ndef parse_host_as_ip(host: str) -> Optional[IPAddress]:\n \"\"\"Interpret a URL host as an IP literal if possible.\n\n ``host`` is the (already bracket stripped) host component. Returns a list of embedded IPv4 addresses for\n IPv4-mapped, IPv4-compatible, 6to4 and Teredo forms; an empty list when\n nothing is embedded. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if not isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address):\n return []\n embedded: List[IPAddress] = []\n if ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n embedded.append(ip.ipv4_mapped)\n if ip.sixtofour is not None:\n embedded.append(ip.sixtofour)\n teredo = ip.teredo\n if teredo is not None:\n embedded.extend([teredo[0], teredo[1]])\n packed = ip.packed\n if packed[:12] == b\"\\x00\" * 12 and int(ip) > 1:\n embedded.append(ipaddress.IPv4Address(packed[12:]))\n return embedded\n\n\ndef _single_address_blocked(ip: IPAddress) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Test one address (without unwrapping) against the denied ranges.\n\n ``ip`` is a parsed address. Returns ``True`` when the address is denied.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if ip in CLOUD_METADATA_ADDRESSES:\n return True\n if (\n ip.is_loopback\n or ip.is_private\n or ip.is_link_local\n or ip.is_multicast\n or ip.is_unspecified\n or ip.is_reserved\n ):\n return True\n if not ip.is_global:\n return True\n for network in BLOCKED_NETWORKS:\n if ip.version == network.version and ip in network:\n return True\n return False\n\n\ndef is_blocked_address(ip: IPAddress) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Test an address, including any embedded IPv4 form, against the denylist.\n\n ``ip`` is a parsed address. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if _single_address_blocked(ip):\n return True\n for embedded in _embedded_addresses(ip):\n if _single_address_blocked(embedded):\n return True\n return False\n\n\ndef resolve_host(host: str) -> List[str]:\n \"\"\"Resolve a hostname to textual addresses using the stdlib resolver.\n\n ``host`` is a DNS name. Returns the list of address strings returned by\n :func:`socket.getaddrinfo` for ``AF_UNSPEC``; returns an empty list when\n resolution fails so callers can fail closed. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n try:\n infos = socket.getaddrinfo(\n host, None, family=socket.AF_UNSPEC, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM\n )\n except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError, ValueError):\n return []\n addresses: List[str] = []\n for info in infos:\n address: Optional[str] = None\n if isinstance(info, str):\n address = info\n else:\n try:\n sockaddr = info[4]\n except (IndexError, TypeError, KeyError):\n sockaddr = None\n if isinstance(sockaddr, (tuple, list)) and sockaddr:\n candidate = sockaddr[0]\n if isinstance(candidate, bytes):\n candidate = candidate.decode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\")\n if isinstance(candidate, str):\n address = candidate\n elif isinstance(sockaddr, str):\n address = sockaddr\n if not address:\n continue\n addresses.append(address.split(\"%\", 1)[0])\n return addresses\n\n\ndef _has_forbidden_characters(url: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Detect control characters or whitespace inside a URL.\n\n ``url`` is the raw client string.
