When disabled no bucket is consulted and no 429 is ever produced, including for per-key quotas.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"10\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\",\n \"purpose\": \"Per-IP creation allowance for POST /api/links inside one window. Also the base for the redirect allowance.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"60\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS\",\n \"purpose\": \"Sliding window length in seconds, shared by the per-IP creation bucket, the per-IP redirect bucket and every per-key bucket.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"100\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_REDIRECT_MULTIPLIER\",\n \"purpose\": \"Multiplier applied to LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX to obtain the per-IP allowance for GET /{code} and GET /api/links/{code}/stats. Unchanged by this work.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"\",\n \"name\": \"SHORTENER_API_KEYS\",\n \"purpose\": \"Comma-separated name:quota pairs, e.g. 'alpha:100,beta:20'. Each entry is trimmed of surrounding whitespace; empty entries are ignored; an entry whose quota is not a base-10 integer >= 1, whose name is empty, or that contains no ':' is logged (name and value redacted) and skipped. Unset or empty means no key is recognised and POST /api/links behaves exactly as today.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"INFO\",\n \"name\": \"LOG_LEVEL\",\n \"purpose\": \"Root log level. Even at DEBUG no code path emits an X-API-Key value or a SHORTENER_API_KEYS entry value.\"\n }\n ],\n \"data_model\": [\n {\n \"fields\": [\n {\n \"constraints\": \"PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, internal only, never exposed in any response\",\n \"name\": \"id\",\n \"type\": \"INTEGER\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NOT NULL, UNIQUE \\u2014 this constraint is the sole enforcer of short-code uniqueness\",\n \"name\": \"code\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NOT NULL, 1..2048 chars, http/https only\",\n \"name\": \"url\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NOT NULL, RFC3339 UTC, defaults to insert time\",\n \"name\": \"created_at\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NULL allowed; NULL means never expires\",\n \"name\": \"expires_at\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n }\n ],\n \"indexes\": [\n \"UNIQUE index on links.code (implicit from the UNIQUE constraint) \\u2014 the redirect hot path is a single equality lookup on code; without it every GET /{code} scans the table\"\n ],\n \"name\": \"links\",\n \"purpose\": \"One row per shortened URL. Schema is frozen: SCHEMA_STATEMENTS is unchanged by this work and PRAGMA table_info(links) must return exactly these columns.\"\n },\n {\n \"fields\": [\n {\n \"constraints\": \"PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT\",\n \"name\": \"id\",\n \"type\": \"INTEGER\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NOT NULL, FOREIGN KEY -> links(id)\",\n \"name\": \"link_id\",\n \"type\": \"INTEGER\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"NOT NULL, RFC3339 UTC\",\n \"name\": \"clicked_at\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n }\n ],\n \"indexes\": [\n \"Index on clicks.link_id \\u2014 GET /api/links/{code}/stats runs COUNT(*) and MAX(clicked_at) filtered by link_id; without it stats scans the whole clicks table\"\n ],\n \"name\": \"clicks\",\n \"purpose\": \"One row per served redirect, aggregated by the stats route. Schema frozen; no new column and no new table is introduced by per-key limiting.\"\n },\n {\n \"fields\": [\n {\n \"constraints\": \"dict key. Exactly one of three namespaced forms: 'post:key:' + hash, 'post:ip:' + hash, 'get:ip:' + hash. Namespacing guarantees a key bucket and an IP bucket can never alias.\",\n \"name\": \"bucket_id\",\n \"type\": \"str\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"hexdigest of blake2b(salt=per-boot secrets.token_bytes(16), data=utf8(api_key or client_host)), truncated to 32 hex chars. The raw key value is never stored.\",\n \"name\": \"hash\",\n \"type\": \"str\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"monotonic timestamps, pruned to the last LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS on every touch; length bounded by the bucket's allowance\",\n \"name\": \"hits\",\n \"type\": \"collections.deque[float]\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \">= 1. For 'post:key:' buckets it is the quota parsed from SHORTENER_API_KEYS for that key; for 'post:ip:' it is LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX; for 'get:ip:' it is LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX * LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_REDIRECT_MULTIPLIER\",\n \"name\": \"allowance\",\n \"type\": \"int\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \"monotonic timestamp used by the existing eviction when the dict exceeds MAX_TRACKED_KEYS\",\n \"name\": \"last_seen\",\n \"type\": \"float\"\n }\n ],\n \"indexes\": [\n \"Python dict keyed by bucket_id \\u2014 O(1) lookup on every request; no scan. Cardinality is bounded by MAX_TRACKED_KEYS (module constant, 10000) plus at most len(SHORTENER_API_KEYS) key buckets, because unrecognised keys never allocate a bucket of their own.\"\n ],\n \"name\": \"RateLimiterState (in-process, app/ratelimit.py, not persisted)\",\n \"purpose\": \"Sliding-window counters held in a single dict guarded by the existing threading.Lock. Vanishes on restart; never written to SQLite, a file or a log.\"\n },\n {\n \"fields\": [\n {\n \"constraints\": \"same per-boot salted blake2b digest used for bucket ids; the plaintext key name from the environment is discarded after hashing\",\n \"name\": \"key_hash\",\n \"type\": \"str\"\n },\n {\n \"constraints\": \">= 1; entries with a non-integer, zero or negative quota, an empty name, or no ':' separator are skipped at parse time\",\n \"name\": \"quota\",\n \"type\": \"int\"\n }\n ],\n \"indexes\": [\n \"Python dict keyed by key_hash \\u2014 one O(1) constant-shape lookup per POST /api/links; a miss is indistinguishable in behaviour and cost from an absent header\"\n ],\n \"name\": \"ApiKeyQuotas (in-process, built once at startup from SHORTENER_API_KEYS)\",\n \"purpose\": \"Frozen mapping from hashed key to quota, consulted by the middleware to decide whether a presented key is recognised.\"\n }\n ],\n \"decisions\": [\n {\n \"choice\": \"Inside the existing RateLimitMiddleware (pure ASGI, added before routing), which inspects scope['method'], scope['path'] and the raw scope['headers'] list for x-api-key and returns a canned 429 JSON response itself.\",\n \"decision\": \"Where per-key throttling is enforced\",\n \"rationale\": \"A caller that blew its quota gets a 429 without the service parsing its body or opening a SQLite connection, so a flood of oversized or malformed POSTs costs almost nothing and can never leave a half-written links row.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"A FastAPI dependency on the create route: runs after routing and body parsing, so a malformed JSON body would produce 422 instead of 429 and would violate the 'no DB connection opened' criterion.\",\n \"A separate middleware class: duplicates window/eviction/lock logic and risks the two limiters disagreeing about the window boundary.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Only for POST /api/links: hash the header value with the per-boot salt, look it up in ApiKeyQuotas; on a hit use bucket 'post:key:'+hash with that key's quota, on a miss (or absent header) use 'post:ip:'+hash(client_host) with LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX. Redirect and stats routes never read the header.\",\n \"decision\": \"How a presented API key selects a bucket\",\n \"rationale\": \"alpha exhausting 100 creations from one address leaves both beta and the anonymous per-IP budget untouched for that same address, and 1000 attacker-invented keys from one address behave exactly like 1000 key-less requests.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Counting key-limited hits in the per-IP bucket as well: a trusted integrator's 100 allowed creations would immediately exhaust the 10-request IP budget for everyone behind that address.\",\n \"Allocating a bucket for every observed key value: unbounded memory growth driven by attacker-controlled input, defeating MAX_TRACKED_KEYS.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"The environment value is parsed at startup into {blake2b(salt, name) -> quota} and the plaintext names are dropped; request header values are hashed on arrival and only the digest is used as a dict key. Parse failures log the entry index and the reason, never the text. No response, no DB column and no log record can contain a key.\",\n \"decision\": \"How key material is kept out of storage and logs\",\n \"rationale\": \"Grepping the sqlite files or DEBUG-level logs for 's3cret-alpha' after a full run finds nothing, so a leaked log bundle does not hand over integrator credentials.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Storing the plaintext key as the dict key: it would appear in any repr/exception traceback of the limiter state.\",\n \"A static salt or a plain unsalted hash: identical digests across boots make an offline dictionary attack against short key names cheap.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Fail soft, exactly like the rest of app/config.py: split on ',', strip whitespace around the entry, the name and the quota, drop empty entries, require exactly one ':' with a non-empty name and a base-10 integer quota >= 1; anything else is logged at WARNING (redacted) and skipped. The process always starts.\",\n \"decision\": \"What happens to malformed SHORTENER_API_KEYS entries\",\n \"rationale\": \"A typo in one integrator's quota does not take the whole shortener down; that integrator simply falls back to the anonymous per-IP limit, which is the safe direction.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Raising at startup: one bad character in an ops-managed env var causes a total outage.\",\n \"Treating quota 0 as 'block this key': introduces a denial path that looks like an auth decision and is indistinguishable to the caller from a normal 429 storm.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"GET /{code} returns 404 with {\\\"error\\\":{\\\"code\\\":\\\"not_found\\\",...}} \\u2014 byte-for-byte the same status, body and headers as a code that never existed. GET /api/links/{code}/stats, which the link's own owner uses, does report expired: true.\",\n \"decision\": \"What a request for an expired code returns\",\n \"rationale\": \"A visitor cannot use the redirect endpoint to learn that some code was once real, so a scraper cannot separate 'not yet issued' from 'issued and lapsed' and narrow its guessing space.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"410 Gone on expiry: confirms the code existed, turning the redirect endpoin\n\nIMPLEMENTATION\n--- README.md ---\n# URL Shortener\n\nPublic JSON API that turns a caller supplied public `http(s)` URL into an\nunguessable base62 short code, redirects visitors until the link expires and\nexposes per-link click analytics that contain no visitor IP addresses.\n\n## Run\n\n```bash\npip install -r requirements.txt\nuvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000\n```\n\n## Endpoints\n\n| Method | Path | Purpose |\n| ------ | ---- | ------- |\n| POST | `/api/links` | Create a short link (`{\"url\": \"...\", \"expires_at\": \"...\"}`) |\n| GET | `/api/links/{code}/stats` | Click analytics (`limit`, `offset`) |\n| GET | `/health` | Liveness; `{\"status\":\"ok\"}` or 503 `{\"status\":\"degraded\"}` |\n| GET | `/{code}` | 307 redirect to the stored destination |\n\nErrors always use `{\"error\": {\"code\": \"...\", \"message\": \"...\"}}`.\n\n## Configuration\n\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n| -------- | ------- | ------- |\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite database file |\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` |\n| `LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS` | `30` | Default link lifetime |\n| `LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH` | `2048` | Maximum accepted URL length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert attempts on code collision |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Creations per client per window |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Rolling window length |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | `false`/`0`/`no` disables the limiter |\n| `LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR` | `false` | Use first `X-Forwarded-For` entry |\n| `LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT` | `50` | Default clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT` | `500` | Maximum clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED` | `true` | Resolve and denylist-check hosts |\n| `LINKS_LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\n\nNo secrets are read or stored by the service.\n\n## Security notes\n\n* Scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`) plus an address denylist covering\n loopback, private, link-local (including `169.254.169.254`), unique-local,\n CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and reserved ranges, with IPv4-mapped,\n IPv4-compatible, 6to4, Teredo and decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals decoded.\n* The service never performs an outbound HTTP request to a destination.\n* Redirect targets come only from the validated, stored row.\n* Rate limiting keys on a per-boot salted hash of the client address; no IP is\n written to the database, to a file or to a log.\n\n\n--- app/__init__.py ---\n\"\"\"URL shortener service package.\n\nExposes :func:`app.main.create_app`, the application factory, and the module\nlevel ``app`` ASGI application used by uvicorn.\n\"\"\"\n\n__all__ = [\"__version__\"]\n\n__version__ = \"1.0.0\"\n\n\n--- app/codes.py ---\n\"\"\"Short code generation.\n\nCodes are drawn from a cryptographically secure random source; they are never\nderived from a database row id or any other sequence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport string\n\nALPHABET = string.ascii_uppercase + string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits\n\n\ndef generate_code(length: int) -> str:\n \"\"\"Generate a base62 short code.\n\n ``length`` is the number of characters to produce. Returns a string of\n exactly ``length`` characters drawn uniformly from ``[A-Za-z0-9]`` using\n :mod:`secrets`. Raises :class:`ValueError` when ``length`` is not positive.\n \"\"\"\n if length <= 0:\n raise ValueError(\"code length must be positive\")\n return \"\".join(secrets.choice(ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\n--- app/config.py ---\n\"\"\"Environment driven configuration for the URL shortener service.\n\nAll settings come from environment variables with the exact names and defaults\nfixed by the design document. No secrets are read or stored here.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Mapping, Optional\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.config\")\n\n#: Hard ceiling applied to the accepted ``url`` length regardless of\n#: ``LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH``. It bounds the string that ever reaches the\n#: database and the pydantic request model.\nABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH = 8192\n\n#: Hard ceiling for the client supplied ``expires_at`` string.\nMAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH = 64\n\n#: Hard ceiling for ``LINKS_BASE_URL``.\nMAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH = 512\n\n_FALSE_VALUES = frozenset({\"false\", \"0\", \"no\"})\n_TRUE_VALUES = frozenset({\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\"})\n\n_VALID_LOG_LEVELS = frozenset(\n {\"CRITICAL\", \"ERROR\", \"WARNING\", \"INFO\", \"DEBUG\", \"NOTSET\"}\n)\n\n\ndef _get(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Read a raw environment value.\n\n Returns the value for ``name`` in ``env`` or ``default`` when the variable\n is missing or empty. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = env.get(name)\n if raw is None:\n return default\n raw = raw.strip()\n if not raw:\n return default\n return raw\n\n\ndef _get_int(\n env: Mapping[str, str],\n name: str,\n default: str,\n minimum: int,\n maximum: int,\n) -> int:\n \"\"\"Read an integer setting, clamped to a safe range.\n\n Returns the parsed integer, or the parsed default when the environment\n value is not an integer or falls outside ``[minimum, maximum]``. Raises\n nothing; an unusable value is logged and the default is used so a typo in\n deployment configuration cannot take the service down.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, default)\n try:\n value = int(raw)\n except ValueError:\n logger.warning(\"Invalid integer for %s; using default %s\", name, default)\n value = int(default)\n if value < minimum or value > maximum:\n logger.warning(\n \"Value for %s outside [%d, %d]; using default %s\",\n name,\n minimum,\n maximum,\n default,\n )\n value = int(default)\n return value\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_true(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to true.\n\n Returns ``False`` only for the case-insensitive values ``false``, ``0`` or\n ``no``; every other value (including absence) yields ``True``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"true\").lower()\n return raw not in _FALSE_VALUES\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_false(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to false.\n\n Returns ``True`` only for the case-insensitive values ``true``, ``1`` or\n ``yes``; every other value (including absence) yields ``False``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"false\").lower()\n return raw in _TRUE_VALUES\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Settings:\n \"\"\"Immutable snapshot of the service configuration.\"\"\"\n\n db_path: str\n base_url: str\n default_ttl_days: int\n max_url_length: int\n code_length: int\n code_max_attempts: int\n rate_limit_max: int\n rate_limit_window_seconds: int\n rate_limit_enabled: bool\n trust_forwarded_for: bool\n stats_default_limit: int\n stats_max_limit: int\n dns_resolution_enabled: bool\n log_level: str\n\n @classmethod\n def from_env(cls, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> \"Settings\":\n \"\"\"Build a :class:`Settings` instance from the process environment.\n\n ``env`` defaults to :data:`os.environ`. Returns a fully populated,\n range-checked settings object. Raises nothing: invalid values fall back\n to the documented defaults.\n \"\"\"\n source: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ if env is None else env\n\n db_path = _get(source, \"LINKS_DB_PATH\", \"./links.db\")\n base_url = _get(source, \"LINKS_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:8000\").rstrip(\"/\")\n if len(base_url) > MAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH:\n logger.warning(\"LINKS_BASE_URL too long; using default\")\n base_url = \"http://localhost:8000\"\n\n max_url_length = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH\", \"2048\", 1, ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH\n )\n stats_max_limit = _get_int(source, \"LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT\", \"500\", 1, 10000)\n stats_default_limit = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT\", \"50\", 1, 10000\n )\n if stats_default_limit > stats_max_limit:\n stats_default_limit = stats_max_limit\n\n log_level = _get(source, \"LINKS_LOG_LEVEL\", \"INFO\").upper()\n if log_level not in _VALID_LOG_LEVELS:\n log_level = \"INFO\"\n\n return cls(\n db_path=db_path,\n base_url=base_url,\n default_ttl_days=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS\", \"30\", 1, 36500\n ),\n max_url_length=max_url_length,\n code_length=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_CODE_LENGTH\", \"7\", 4, 32),\n code_max_attempts=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS\", \"5\", 1, 100\n ),\n rate_limit_max=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\", \"10\", 1, 1000000),\n rate_limit_window_seconds=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS\", \"60\", 1, 86400\n ),\n rate_limit_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED\"\n ),\n trust_forwarded_for=_get_bool_default_false(\n source, \"LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR\"\n ),\n stats_default_limit=stats_default_limit,\n stats_max_limit=stats_max_limit,\n dns_resolution_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED\"\n ),\n log_level=log_level,\n )\n\n\n--- app/db.py ---\n\"\"\"SQLite access helpers.\n\nOne connection per request; WAL mode so a click insert does not block\nconcurrent redirect reads. Every statement in the service is parameterized.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.db\")\n\nSCHEMA_STATEMENTS = (\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n url TEXT NOT NULL,\n created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n expires_at TEXT NOT NULL\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clicks (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n link_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES links(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n clicked_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n referrer TEXT,\n user_agent TEXT\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clicks_link_time\n ON clicks(link_id, clicked_at DESC, id DESC)\n \"\"\",\n)\n\n\ndef connect(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n \"\"\"Open a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Returns a connection with\n ``sqlite3.Row`` rows, foreign keys enforced, WAL journalling and a 5 second\n busy timeout. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n try:\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL\")\n except sqlite3.DatabaseError: # pragma: no cover - filesystem dependent\n logger.warning(\"WAL journal mode unavailable; continuing with default\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL\")\n return conn\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef get_connection(db_path: str) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n \"\"\"Context manager yielding a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Yields the connection and always\n closes it. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = connect(db_path)\n try:\n yield conn\n finally:\n conn.close()\n\n\ndef init_db(db_path: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create the schema if it does not exist.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path; its parent directory is created when\n missing. Returns nothing. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` or :class:`OSError`\n when the database or its directory cannot be created.\n \"\"\"\n parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n if parent:\n os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)\n with get_connection(db_path) as conn:\n for statement in SCHEMA_STATEMENTS:\n conn.execute(statement)\n conn.commit()\n\n\n--- app/errors.py ---\n\"\"\"Stable error envelope shared by every endpoint.\n\nAll failures are rendered as ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\nNo stack trace, database message or filesystem path is ever placed in a\nresponse body.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Mapping, Optional\n\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\n\nMAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 400\n\n\nclass ApiError(Exception):\n \"\"\"Application level error carrying an HTTP status and a stable code.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(\n self,\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n ) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create an API error.\n\n ``status_code`` is the HTTP status to return, ``code`` the stable\n machine readable identifier, ``message`` a human readable, non\n sensitive explanation and ``headers`` optional extra response headers.\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n super().__init__(message)\n self.status_code = status_code\n self.code = code\n self.message = message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]\n self.headers: Dict[str, str] = dict(headers or {})\n\n\ndef error_payload(code: str, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Build the canonical error body.\n\n Returns a dict of the shape ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return {\"error\": {\"code\": code, \"message\": message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]}}\n\n\ndef error_response(\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Build a JSON error response using the canonical envelope.\n\n Returns a :class:`fastapi.responses.JSONResponse` with ``status_code`` and\n the given headers. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return JSONResponse(\n status_code=status_code,\n content=error_payload(code, message),\n headers=dict(headers or {}),\n )\n\n\n--- app/main.py ---\n\"\"\"FastAPI application for the URL shortener service.\n\nEndpoints:\n POST /api/links create a short link\n GET /api/links/{code}/stats per-link click analytics\n GET /health liveness probe\n GET /{code} redirect to the stored destination\n\nThe redirect destination always comes from the validated, stored row; it can\nnever come from a query parameter, header or path segment of the request.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\nfrom typing import Any, AsyncIterator, Dict, List, Optional\nfrom urllib.parse import quote\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Query, Request, Response\nfrom fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException\n\nfrom . import codes as codes_module\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .db import get_connection, init_db\nfrom .errors import ApiError, error_response\nfrom .models import CreateLinkRequest, CreateLinkResponse, HealthResponse, StatsResponse\nfrom .ratelimit import RateLimitMiddleware\nfrom .validation import validate_destination_url\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links\")\n\nTIMESTAMP_FORMAT = \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%fZ\"\nMAX_HEADER_STORE_LENGTH = 2048\n_LOCATION_SAFE_CHARACTERS = \":/?#[]@!$&'()*+,;=%~-._\"\n\n\nclass ClientAddressRedactionFilter(logging.Filter):\n \"\"\"Logging filter that removes the client address from access log records.\"\"\"\n\n def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Replace the client address argument of an access log record.\n\n ``record`` is the log record being emitted. ORDER BY clicked_at DESC, id DESC \"\n \"LIMIT ? Raises :class:`ApiError` 404 ``not_found``\n for unknown or malformed codes and 410 ``link_expired`` for lapsed\n links; neither writes a click row.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n pattern: re.Pattern[str] = request.app.state.code_pattern\n if not pattern.match(code):\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT id, url, expires_at FROM links WHERE code = ?\",\n (code,),\n ).fetchone()\n if row is None:\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n try:\n expires = parse_timestamp(row[\"expires_at\"])\n except ValueError:\n # Fail closed: an unreadable expiry is treated as expired.\n logger.error(\"Unparsable expires_at for a stored link\")\n raise ApiError(410, \"link_expired\", \"This link has expired.\")\n\n if now >= expires:\n raise ApiError(410, \"link_expired\", \"This link has expired.\")\n\n conn.execute(\n \"INSERT INTO clicks (link_id, clicked_at, referrer, user_agent) \"\n \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n (\n row[\"id\"],\n format_timestamp(now),\n clip_header(request.headers.get(\"referer\")),\n clip_header(request.headers.get(\"user-agent\")),\n ),\n )\n conn.commit()\n destination = row[\"url\"]\n\n return Response(\n status_code=307,\n headers={\n \"Location\": location_header_value(destination),\n \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\",\n \"Referrer-Policy\": \"no-referrer\",\n },\n )\n\n return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\": # pragma: no cover - manual entry point\n import uvicorn\n\n uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", host=\"127.0.0.1\", port=8000, reload=False)\n\n\n--- app/models.py ---\n\"\"\"Typed request and response models validated at the HTTP boundary.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, StringConstraints\nfrom typing_extensions import Annotated\n\nfrom .config import ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH, MAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH\n\nUrlStr = Annotated[\n str,\n StringConstraints(strict=True, min_length=1, max_length=ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH),\n]\n\nExpiryStr = Annotated[\n str,\n StringConstraints(strict=True, min_length=1, max_length=MAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH),\n]\n\n\nclass CreateLinkRequest(BaseModel):\n \"\"\"Body of ``POST /api/links``.\"\"\"\n\n model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"forbid\")\n\n url: UrlStr = Field(\n ...,\n description=\"Public http(s) destination URL to shorten.\",\n )\n expires_at: Optional[ExpiryStr] = Field(\n default=None,\n description=\"Optional ISO-8601 expiry instant; must be in the future.\",\n )\n\n\nclass CreateLinkResponse(BaseModel):\n \"\"\"Body returned by a successful ``POST /api/links``.\"\"\"\n\n code: str\n short_url: str\n url: str\n created_at: str\n expires_at: str\n\n\nclass ClickEntry(BaseModel):\n \"\"\"A single recorded redirect, free of any client address.\"\"\"\n\n timestamp: str\n referrer: Optional[str] = None\n user_agent: Optional[str] = None\n\n\nclass StatsResponse(BaseModel):\n \"\"\"Body returned by ``GET /api/links/{code}/stats``.\"\"\"\n\n code: str\n url: str\n created_at: str\n expires_at: str\n total_clicks: int\n clicks: List[ClickEntry]\n\n\nclass HealthResponse(BaseModel):\n \"\"\"Body returned by ``GET /health``.\"\"\"\n\n status: str\n\n\n--- app/ratelimit.py ---\n\"\"\"In-process sliding window rate limiting, applied as ASGI middleware.\n\nThe limiter runs before routing and body parsing, so a flood is rejected\nwithout touching the database. Keys are a per-boot salted SHA-256 of the client\naddress: the address itself is never stored, persisted or logged, and all state\nvanishes on restart.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport math\nimport re\nimport secrets\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom collections import OrderedDict, deque\nfrom typing import Deque, Optional, Tuple\n\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .errors import error_response\n\n#: Redirects are far cheaper and far more frequent than creations, so the\n#: redirect bucket uses a multiple of the configured creation budget. The\n#: design pins only the creation limit; this keeps redirects protected without\n#: introducing a new environment variable.\nREDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER = 100\n\n#: Upper bound on tracked keys so the limiter cannot grow without limit.\nMAX_TRACKED_KEYS = 20000\n\nCREATE_PATH = \"/api/links\"\n\n\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\n \"\"\"Rate limit link creation and redirects in a single place.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings) -> None:\n \"\"\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\n\n ``app`` is the downstream ASGI application and ``settings`` the service\n configuration. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self.app = app\n self.settings = settings\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(32)\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\n self._hits: \"OrderedDict[str, Deque[float]]\" = OrderedDict()\n self._code_pattern = re.compile(\n r\"^/[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\" % settings.code_length\n )\n\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n \"\"\"ASGI entry point.\n\n ``scope``, ``receive`` and ``send`` are the standard ASGI arguments.\n Returns nothing; either forwards the call downstream or emits a 429 with\n a ``Retry-After`` header. Raises nothing of its own.\n \"\"\"\n if scope.get(\"type\") != \"http\" or not self.settings.rate_limit_enabled:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n rule = self._rule_for(str(scope.get(\"method\", \"\")), str(scope.get(\"path\", \"\")))\n if rule is None:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n bucket, limit = rule\n allowed, retry_after = self._consume(bucket, self._client_key(scope, bucket), limit)\n if allowed:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n response = error_response(\n 429,\n \"rate_limited\",\n \"Too many requests. Please retry later.\",\n headers={\"Retry-After\": str(retry_after), \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\"},\n )\n await response(scope, receive, send)\n\n def _rule_for(self, method: str, path: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, int]]:\n \"\"\"Decide which limiter bucket applies to a request.\n\n ``method`` is the HTTP method and ``path`` the request path. Returns a\n ``(bucket, limit)`` pair for rate limited routes, or ``None`` when the\n route is not limited. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n normalized = path.rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n if method.upper() == \"POST\" and normalized == CREATE_PATH:\n return \"create\", self.settings.rate_limit_max\n if method.upper() in {\"GET\", \"HEAD\"} and self._code_pattern.match(path):\n return \"redirect\", self.settings.rate_limit_max * REDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER\n return None\n\n def _client_address(self, scope: Scope) -> str:\n \"\"\"Determine the client address used for keying.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope. Returns the first ``X-Forwarded-For`` entry\n when the service is configured to trust it, otherwise the peer address,\n or ``\"unknown\"``. The value is only hashed, never stored or logged.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if self.settings.trust_forwarded_for:\n for name, value in scope.get(\"headers\") or []:\n if name == b\"x-forwarded-for\":\n first = value.decode(\"latin-1\", \"ignore\").split(\",\")[0].strip()\n if first:\n return first[:128]\n break\n client = scope.get(\"client\")\n if client:\n try:\n return str(client[0])[:128]\n except (IndexError, TypeError): # pragma: no cover - defensive\n return \"unknown\"\n return \"unknown\"\n\n def _client_key(self, scope: Scope, bucket: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Build the salted hash key for a client and bucket.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope and ``bucket`` the limiter bucket name.\n Returns a hex SHA-256 digest that cannot be reversed to an IP address.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n digest = hashlib.sha256()\n digest.update(self._salt)\n digest.update(bucket.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n digest.update(b\"\\x00\")\n digest.update(self._client_address(scope).encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n return digest.hexdigest()\n\n def _consume(self, bucket: str, key: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n \"\"\"Record a request against the rolling window.\n\n ``bucket`` is the limiter bucket (used only for readability), ``key``\n the hashed client key and ``limit`` the allowed number of requests per\n window. Returns ``(allowed, retry_after_seconds)`` where\n ``retry_after_seconds`` is at least 1 when the request is denied.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n now = time.monotonic()\n window = float(self.settings.rate_limit_window_seconds)\n with self._lock:\n hits = self._hits.get(key)\n if hits is None:\n hits = deque()\n self._hits[key] = hits\n self._hits.move_to_end(key)\n while hits and (now - hits[0]) >= window:\n hits.popleft()\n if len(hits) >= limit:\n retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(window - (now - hits[0])))\n return False, retry_after\n hits.append(now)\n self._prune_locked()\n return True, 0\n\n def _prune_locked(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Drop stale limiter state while the lock is held.\n\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if len(self._hits) <= MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n return\n for stale_key in [k for k, v in self._hits.items() if not v]:\n self._hits.pop(stale_key, None)\n while len(self._hits) > MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n self._hits.popitem(last=False)\n\n\n--- app/validation.py ---\n\"\"\"Destination URL validation.\n\nOnly ``http`` and ``https`` URLs are accepted (allow-list, not a block-list of\nbad schemes). Hosts are resolved and every resulting address is checked against\nthe denied ranges: loopback, private, link-local (including the cloud metadata\naddress 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and\nother reserved space. alpha from one client address all return 201, and the 101st within the same 60 second window returns 429.\n- With the same configuration, 20 POST /api/links requests carrying X-API-Key: beta return 201 and the 21st returns 429, proving each key uses its own quota value.\n- A 429 produced by the per-key limiter has body {\"error\":{\"code\":\"rate_limited\",\"message\":...}}, a Retry-After header whose value parses as an integer >= 1 and <= 60, and adds no row to the links table.\n- After key alpha (quota 100) has been exhausted from client address A, the first POST /api/links carrying X-API-Key: beta from the same address A returns 201: buckets are keyed by API key, not by address, for known keys.\n- After 15 successful POST /api/links requests carrying X-API-Key: alpha from client address A, a POST /api/links from address A with no X-API-Key header returns 201, showing key-limited requests never consumed the per-IP creation budget.\n- With SHORTENER_API_KEYS unset or empty, POST /api/links behaviour is identical to today: the 11th creation from one address within the window returns 429 with Retry-After and the existing tests/test_rate_limit.py suite passes unchanged.\n- A POST /api/links carrying X-API-Key: gamma (a name absent from SHORTENER_API_KEYS) is counted in the same per-IP creation bucket as key-less requests: 10 key-less creations followed by one gamma-keyed creation from the same address returns 429 for the gamma request.\n- 1000 POST /api/links requests from one address each carrying a distinct random X-API-Key value produce the same per-IP throttling as 1000 key-less requests and create no additional limiter buckets beyond the per-IP one.\n- GET /{code} requests carrying X-API-Key: alpha where alpha's quota is 1 still succeed with 307 for at least five consecutive visits: redirects are never limited by key and remain on the existing per-IP redirect bucket (LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX * 100).\n- GET /api/links/{code}/stats and GET /health are unaffected by X-API-Key: presence of any key value changes neither status code nor headers for these routes.\n- With LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED=false and SHORTENER_API_KEYS=\"alpha:2\", 15 POST /api/links requests carrying X-API-Key: alpha all return 201.\n- SHORTENER_API_KEYS=\" alpha : 100 , beta:20 ,\" parses to exactly the two quotas 100 and 20 (surrounding whitespace and empty trailing entries tolerated).\n- SHORTENER_API_KEYS=\"alpha:abc,noquota,:5,beta:0,gamma:7\" causes the malformed entries to be discarded while gamma:7 is enforced; the service starts normally and requests with X-API-Key: alpha are treated as unknown (per-IP limit).\n- Running the whole flow with a key value of \"s3cret-alpha\" leaves that string absent from the sqlite database files on disk and from captured log output at DEBUG level.\n- PRAGMA table_info(links) and PRAGMA table_info(clicks) return the same columns as before the change and no new table exists in the database after exercising the key limiter.\n- A per-key 429 is emitted by the ASGI middleware before request body parsing and before any database connection is opened for that request (no links row, no clicks row, and the response is produced even when the JSON body is malformed).\n- Retry-After on a per-key 429 equals the whole number of seconds, rounded up and at least 1, until the oldest recorded hit for that key leaves the 60 second window.\n\nWrite the pytest suite for this implementation. Import paths, endpoint paths, field names and status codes must match the implementation exactly, not the design, when the two differ. Write pytest that fails against a plausible wrong implementation. window rate limiting, enforced in one ASGI middleware.\n\nThe middleware runs before routing, before body parsing and before any\ndatabase connection is opened, so a throttled request costs almost Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n client = scope.get(\"client\")\n if isinstance(client, (tuple, list)) and client:\n host = client[0]\n if host:\n return str(host)\n return \"unknown\"\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n \"\"\"In-process sliding window limiter shared by every limited route.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(\n self,\n *,\n enabled: bool,\n window_seconds: int,\n create_max: int,\n redirect_multiplier: int,\n api_key_entries: Tuple[Tuple[str, int], ...] = (),\n ) -> None:\n \"\"\"Build a limiter.\n\n Hashes the configured API key names with a per-boot salt and discards\n the plaintext immediately, so no credential survives in memory.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self._enabled = bool(enabled)\n self._window = float(max(1, window_seconds))\n self._create_max = max(1, create_max)\n self._read_max = max(1, create_max * max(1, redirect_multiplier))\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(16)\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\n self._buckets: Dict[str, _Bucket] = {}\n quotas: Dict[str, int] = {}\n for name, quota in api_key_entries:\n if quota < 1:\n continue\n quotas[self.digest(name)] = int(quota)\n self._key_quotas: Dict[str, int] = quotas\n if quotas:\n LOGGER.info(\"api key quotas loaded for %d key(s)\", len(quotas))\n\n @property\n def enabled(self) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Report whether limiting is active.\n\n Returns ``True`` when buckets are consulted. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return self._enabled\n\n @property\n def window_seconds(self) -> float:\n \"\"\"Return the sliding window length in seconds. Raises nothing.\"\"\"\n return self._window\n\n @property\n def create_allowance(self) -> int:\n \"\"\"Return the per-IP creation allowance. Raises nothing.\"\"\"\n return self._create_max\n\n @property\n def read_allowance(self) -> int:\n \"\"\"Return the per-IP read/redirect allowance. Raises nothing.\"\"\"\n return self._read_max\n\n def digest(self, value: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Hash a credential or address with the per-boot salt.\n\n Returns a 32 character hex digest. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return hashlib.blake2b(\n value.encode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"), salt=self._salt, digest_size=16\n ).hexdigest()\n\n def resolve_key(self, presented: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Tuple[str, int]]:\n \"\"\"Look up a presented API key value.\n\n Returns ``(key_hash, quota)`` when the key is recognised, otherwise\n ``None`` - an unknown key is indistinguishable from no key at all.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if presented is None or not self._key_quotas:\n return None\n key_hash = self.digest(presented)\n quota = self._key_quotas.get(key_hash)\n if quota is None:\n return None\n return key_hash, quota\n\n def bucket_ids(self) -> Tuple[str, ...]:\n \"\"\"Return a snapshot of the currently tracked bucket ids.\n\n Intended for diagnostics and tests only. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n with self._lock:\n return tuple(self._buckets.keys())\n\n def bucket_count(self) -> int:\n \"\"\"Return how many buckets are currently tracked. Raises nothing.\"\"\"\n with self._lock:\n return len(self._buckets)\n\n def reset(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Drop all counters.\n\n Returns ``None``. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n with self._lock:\n self._buckets.clear()\n\n def _evict_locked(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Evict least recently seen IP buckets when the dict is full.\n\n Key buckets are never evicted because their cardinality is bounded by\n the configuration. Must be called with the lock held. Returns\n ``None``. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if len(self._buckets) < MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n return\n evictable: List[Tuple[float, str]] = [\n (bucket.last_seen, bucket_id)\n for bucket_id, bucket in self._buckets.items()\n if not bucket_id.startswith(KEY_BUCKET_PREFIX)\n ]\n if not evictable:\n return\n evictable.sort()\n excess = len(self._buckets) - MAX_TRACKED_KEYS + 1\n for _, bucket_id in evictable[:excess]:\n self._buckets.pop(bucket_id, None)\n\n def consume(self, bucket_id: str, allowance: int) -> Decision:\n \"\"\"Record one hit against a bucket if the allowance permits it.\n\n Returns a :class:`Decision`; when it is not allowed, ``retry_after`` is\n the whole number of seconds (rounded up, at least 1, never more than\n the window) until the oldest recorded hit leaves the window. A denied\n request is not recorded, so denials never extend the window.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n effective = max(1, allowance)\n now = time.monotonic()\n cutoff = now - self._window\n with self._lock:\n bucket = self._buckets.get(bucket_id)\n if bucket is None:\n self._evict_locked()\n bucket = _Bucket(hits=deque(), allowance=effective, last_seen=now)\n self._buckets[bucket_id] = bucket\n bucket.allowance = effective\n bucket.last_seen = now\n while bucket.hits and bucket.hits[0] <= cutoff:\n bucket.hits.popleft()\n if len(bucket.hits) >= effective:\n oldest = bucket.hits[0]\n remaining = (oldest + self._window) - now\n retry_after = int(math.ceil(remaining)) if remaining > 0 else 1\n retry_after = max(1, min(retry_after, int(math.ceil(self._window))))\n return Decision(allowed=False, retry_after=retry_after)\n bucket.hits.append(now)\n return Decision(allowed=True, retry_after=0)\n\n def check_request(self, scope: Scope) -> Decision:\n \"\"\"Apply the limiter to one HTTP scope.\n\n Chooses the per-key creation bucket for a recognised ``X-API-Key`` on\n ``POST /api/links``, the per-IP creation bucket otherwise, and the\n per-IP read bucket for redirect and stats requests. Unlimited routes\n always return an allowed decision. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if not self._enabled:\n return Decision(allowed=True, retry_after=0)\n method = str(scope.get(\"method\", \"\"))\n path = str(scope.get(\"path\", \"/\"))\n category = classify_request(method, path)\n if category is None:\n return Decision(allowed=True, retry_after=0)\n if category == CATEGORY_CREATE:\n headers = scope.get(\"headers\") or []\n resolved = self.resolve_key(extract_api_key(headers))\n if resolved is not None:\n key_hash, quota = resolved\n return self.consume(KEY_BUCKET_PREFIX + key_hash, quota)\n host_hash = self.digest(client_host_of(scope))\n return self.consume(IP_CREATE_BUCKET_PREFIX + host_hash, self._create_max)\n host_hash = self.digest(client_host_of(scope))\n return self.consume(IP_READ_BUCKET_PREFIX + host_hash, self._read_max)\n\n\nasync def send_rate_limited(send: Send, retry_after: int) -> None:\n \"\"\"Emit the canned 429 response.\n\n Returns ``None``. Raises whatever the ASGI ``send`` callable raises.\n \"\"\"\n headers = [\n (b\"content-type\", b\"application/json\"),\n (b\"content-length\", str(len(_RATE_LIMITED_BODY)).encode(\"ascii\")),\n (b\"retry-after\", str(max(1, retry_after)).encode(\"ascii\")),\n (b\"cache-control\", b\"no-store\"),\n ]\n await send({\"type\": \"http.response.start\", \"status\": 429, \"headers\": headers})\n await send({\"type\": \"http.response.body\", \"body\": _RATE_LIMITED_BODY})\n\n\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\n \"\"\"Pure ASGI middleware enforcing every rate limit in one place.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, limiter: RateLimiter) -> None:\n \"\"\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\n\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self.app = app\n self.limiter = limiter\n\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n \"\"\"Handle one ASGI event stream.\n\n Consults the limiter before routing and body parsing; on refusal emits\n a 429 with ``Retry-After`` and ``Cache-Control: no-store`` and never\n calls the wrapped application. Returns ``None``. Raises whatever the\n wrapped application raises.\n \"\"\"\n if scope.get(\"type\") != \"http\" or not self.limiter.enabled:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n decision = self.limiter.check_request(scope)\n if not decision.allowed:\n await send_rate_limited(send, decision.retry_after)\n return\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n", "path": "app/ratelimit.py"}, touches no user data |\n\nEvery error uses one shape:\n\n```json\n{\"error\": {\"code\": \"rate_limited\", \"message\": \"Rate limit exceeded. Please retry later.\"}}\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------- |\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite file path |\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` (trailing slash stripped) |\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length; values outside 4..16 log and fall back |\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert retries on code collision before 503 |\n| `LINKS_MAX_TTL_SECONDS` | `31536000` | Upper bound for `expires_in_seconds` |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | Master switch; `false/0/no/off` disable |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Per-IP creation allowance per window |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Sliding window length |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_REDIRECT_MULTIPLIER` | `100` | Read allowance = max * multiplier |\n| `SHORTENER_API_KEYS` | (empty) | `name:quota` pairs, comma separated |\n| `LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\n\nAPI keys are credentials: they are hashed with a per-boot salt on arrival and\nnever written to the database, a log record or a response body.\n", "path": "README.md"}], "notes": "Scope: the design describes only the per-key limiting delta, so the rest of the service (schema, routes, URL validation, error envelope) is implemented from scratch to match the design's stated behaviour and the platform rules.\n\nKey decisions where the design was silent:\n- Validation failure statuses: 400 with code `invalid_url` for a rejected target URL; 422 with `invalid_request` for pydantic/body failures and for `expires_in_seconds` above `LINKS_MAX_TTL_SECONDS` (the design explicitly says a malformed body yields 422, so I kept 422 for all body-shape failures).\n- Short code allocation failure after `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` returns 503 `service_unavailable`, as the design requires 503.\n- Rate-limit categorisation is path-shape based inside the middleware: `POST /api/links` -> create bucket; `GET /{single-segment}` (excluding health/docs/redoc/openapi.json/favicon.ico) and `GET /api/links/{code}/stats` -> the per-IP read bucket. `/health` is never limited and never reads the DB.\n- Client identity is the ASGI transport peer (`scope['client']`), never `X-Forwarded-For`, because forwarding headers are client-forgeable. Missing peer maps to the literal `unknown`.\n- `X-API-Key` values longer than 512 bytes or not valid UTF-8 are treated as absent (fall back to the per-IP bucket), which keeps attacker-controlled input from allocating buckets and bounds hashing work.\n- Denied requests are not recorded as hits, so `Retry-After` is exactly ceil(seconds until the oldest recorded hit leaves the window), clamped to [1, window]; a 429 storm cannot extend a caller's penalty.\n- Bucket eviction at MAX_TRACKED_KEYS (10000) never evicts `post:key:` buckets, matching the design's stated cardinality bound of MAX_TRACKED_KEYS + len(keys).\n- Duplicate names in SHORTENER_API_KEYS: last entry wins.\n\nSecurity behaviour worth reviewing first:\n- app/urls.py resolves every non-literal host via getaddrinfo and rejects the request when resolution fails. This is the safe direction but means creating a link requires working DNS for the target host; literal-IP targets need no DNS. Resolution verdicts are cached for 30s (max 1024 hosts) so a burst of creations does not repeat lookups.\n- Only the stored, validated URL is ever used as a redirect destination; there is no code path where a query parameter, header or path segment becomes a redirect target.\n- Settings.api_key_entries is declared `repr=False` so plaintext key names cannot appear in a dataclass repr or traceback frame; RateLimiter hashes them at construction with a per-boot 16-byte salt and keeps only digests.\n\nOther notes:\n- requirements.txt pins the three libraries the constraints name, plus uvicorn (needed to actually run the ASGI app) and httpx/pytest (Starlette's TestClient requires httpx). No runtime import outside the pinned set.\n- I did not add any files under tests/ because test authoring runs concurrently and I must not collide with the generated suite.\n- SCHEMA_STATEMENTS creates exactly `links` and `clicks` plus the documented index on clicks.link_id; no table, column or index is added by the key limiter."}, "prompt": "INTENT\nLink-creation callers that present a recognised API key are throttled against that key's own per-minute quota instead of the shared per-IP budget, so trusted integrators get a higher creation allowance while every existing anonymous client keeps exactly today's per-IP behaviour.\n\nDESIGN\n{\n \"configuration\": [\n {\n \"default\": \"./links.db\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_DB_PATH\",\n \"purpose\": \"Filesystem path to the SQLite database file. This is the only name the service reads and the only name tests may set; DATABASE_PATH is not read.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"http://localhost:8000\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_BASE_URL\",\n \"purpose\": \"Origin used to build the short_url field in POST /api/links responses. Parsed case-insensitively; 'false','0','no','off' disable it. When disabled no bucket is consulted and no 429 is ever produced, including for per-key quotas.\"\n },\n {\n \"default\": \"10\",\n \"name\": \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\",\n \"purpose\": \"Per-IP creation allowance for POST /api/links inside one window. on a hit use bucket 'post:key:'+hash with that key's quota, on a miss (or absent header) use 'post:ip:'+hash(client_host) with LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX. Redirect and stats routes never read the header.\",\n \"decision\": \"How a presented API key selects a bucket\",\n \"rationale\": \"alpha exhausting 100 creations from one address leaves both beta and the anonymous per-IP budget untouched for that same address, and 1000 attacker-invented keys from one address behave exactly like 1000 key-less requests.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Counting key-limited hits in the per-IP bucket as well: a trusted integrator's 100 allowed creations would immediately exhaust the 10-request IP budget for everyone behind that address.\",\n \"Allocating a bucket for every observed key value: unbounded memory growth driven by attacker-controlled input, defeating MAX_TRACKED_KEYS.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"The environment value is parsed at startup into {blake2b(salt, name) -> quota} and the plaintext names are dropped; request header values are hashed on arrival and only the digest is used as a dict key. Parse failures log the entry index and the reason, never the text. No response, no DB column and no log record can contain a key.\",\n \"decision\": \"How key material is kept out of storage and logs\",\n \"rationale\": \"Grepping the sqlite files or DEBUG-level logs for 's3cret-alpha' after a full run finds nothing, so a leaked log bundle does not hand over integrator credentials.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Storing the plaintext key as the dict key: it would appear in any repr/exception traceback of the limiter state.\",\n \"A static salt or a plain unsalted hash: identical digests across boots make an offline dictionary attack against short key names cheap.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Fail soft, exactly like the rest of app/config.py: split on ',', strip whitespace around the entry, the name and the quota, drop empty entries, require exactly one ':' with a non-empty name and a base-10 integer quota >= 1; anything else is logged at WARNING (redacted) and skipped. The process always starts.\",\n \"decision\": \"What happens to malformed SHORTENER_API_KEYS entries\",\n \"rationale\": \"A typo in one integrator's quota does not take the whole shortener down; that integrator simply falls back to the anonymous per-IP limit, which is the safe direction.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Raising at startup: one bad character in an ops-managed env var causes a total outage.\",\n \"Treating quota 0 as 'block this key': introduces a denial path that looks like an auth decision and is indistinguishable to the caller from a normal 429 storm.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"GET /{code} returns 404 with {\\\"error\\\":{\\\"code\\\":\\\"not_found\\\",...}} \\u2014 byte-for-byte the same status, body and headers as a code that never existed. GET /api/links/{code}/stats, which the link's own owner uses, does report expired: true.\",\n \"decision\": \"What a request for an expired code returns\",\n \"rationale\": \"A visitor cannot use the {\"error\":{\"code\":\"rate_limited\",\"message\":...}}, a Retry-After header whose value parses as an integer >= 1 and <= 60, and adds no row to the links table.\n- After key alpha (quota 100) has successful POST /api/links requests carrying X-API-Key: alpha from client address A, a POST /api/links from address A with no X-API-Key header returns 201, showing key-limited requests never consumed case-sensitively).\n- Per-key limiting applies only to POST /api/links; GET /{code} redirect throttling, its multiplier and its per-IP keying must remain byte-for-byte behaviourally identical.\n- keep the existing envelope {\"error\":{\"code\":\"rate_limited\",\"message\":...}} with Cache-Control: no-store and Retry-After in whole seconds >= 1.\n- API keys are credentials: the key value must never be written to the database, to a file, to a log record or to any response body, and limiter state must key on a per-boot salted hash of the key, mirroring the existing treatment of client addresses.\n- Key comparison and lookup must not leak whether a key exists through the response: an unknown key yields the same status and body shape as no key at all (no 401/403 path is introduced).\n- Unknown or attacker-supplied key values must not be able to grow limiter memory: such requests fall back to the per-IP bucket, and the existing MAX_TRACKED_KEYS bound on tracked You write FastAPI on SQLite. When the design is silent on something you need, choose the safe option and record the choice in notes. When the design is wrong in a way that would ship a defect, implement it correctly and say what you changed and why in notes. Do not silently diverge.\n\nNon negotiable, because a gate checks each of these and a reviewer reads the diff after you:\n\nValidate at the boundary with typed request models. Reject invalid input, do not coerce it. Length limits on every string that reaches the database.\n\nEvery user supplied target URL is validated before it is stored and the stored value is what is later served, so nothing that skipped validation can be reached. Allow http and https only, and reject javascript:, data:, file: and every other scheme rather than blocking a list of known bad ones. Resolve the host and reject loopback, private, link local, multicast, reserved and unspecified addresses, with 169.254.169.254 denied explicitly because that address is the cloud metadata endpoint and reaching it is how a URL fetcher becomes a credential leak. Reject embedded credentials in the URL.\n\nOpen redirect defence: the service redirects only to a target it validated and stored itself. A redirect destination must never come from a query parameter, a header or a path segment of the incoming request.\n\nRate limiting on link creation and on redirects, enforced in one place rather than repeated per route, keyed on something the client cannot trivially forge, returning 429 with a Retry-After header.\n\nLink expiry enforced on the read path, not only at creation, and expired links behave exactly as the design says.\n\nA health endpoint that reports liveness without touching user data.\n\nConfiguration from environment variables with safe defaults. No secrets, keys, tokens, passwords or connection strings in code, including realistic looking placeholders.\n\nParameterized SQL only. Building a query with string formatting or f strings is a defect even when the input looks safe.\n\nNever use eval, exec, os.system, subprocess with shell=True, or pickle on data that came from a request. before any\\ndatabase connection is opened, so a throttled request costs almost nothing and\\ncan never leave a half written row.\\n\\nBuckets are namespaced so a key bucket and an IP bucket can never alias:\\n\\n* ``post:key:<hash>`` - per API key creation quota (recognised keys only)\\n* ``post:ip:<hash>`` - per client address creation quota\\n* ``get:ip:<hash>`` - per client address touches no user data |\\n\\nEvery error uses one shape:\\n\\n```json\\n{\\\"error\\\": {\\\"code\\\": \\\"rate_limited\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Rate limit exceeded. Please retry later.\\\"}}\\n```\\n\\n## Configuration\\n\\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\\n| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------- |\\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite file path |\\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` (trailing slash stripped) |\\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length; values outside 4..16 log and fall back |\\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert retries on code collision before 503 |\\n| `LINKS_MAX_TTL_SECONDS` | `31536000` | Upper bound for `expires_in_seconds` |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | Master switch; `false/0/no/off` disable |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Per-IP creation allowance per window |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Sliding window length |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_REDIRECT_MULTIPLIER` | `100` | Read allowance = max * multiplier |\\n| `SHORTENER_API_KEYS` | (empty) | `name:quota` pairs, comma separated |\\n| `LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\\n\\nAPI keys are credentials: they are hashed with a per-boot salt on arrival and\\nnever written to the database, a log record or a response body.\\n\"}],\"notes\":\"Scope: the design describes only the per-key limiting delta, so the rest of the service (schema, routes, URL validation, error envelope) is implemented from scratch to match the design's stated behaviour and the platform rules.\\n\\nKey decisions where the design was silent:\\n- Validation failure statuses: 400 with code `invalid_url` for a rejected target URL; 422 with `invalid_request` for pydantic/body failures and for `expires_in_seconds` above `LINKS_MAX_TTL_SECONDS` (the design explicitly says a malformed body yields 422, so I kept 422 for all body-shape failures).\\n- Short code allocation failure after `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` returns 503 `service_unavailable`, as the design requires 503.\\n- Rate-limit categorisation is path-shape based inside the middleware: `POST /api/links` -> create bucket; `GET /{single-segment}` (excluding health/docs/redoc/openapi.json/favicon.ico) and `GET /api/links/{code}/stats` -> the per-IP read bucket. `/health` is never limited and never reads the DB.\\n- Client identity is the ASGI transport peer (`scope['client']`), never `X-Forwarded-For`, because forwarding headers are client-forgeable. Missing peer maps to the literal `unknown`.\\n- `X-API-Key` values longer than 512 bytes or not valid UTF-8 are treated as absent (fall back to the per-IP bucket), which keeps attacker-controlled input from allocating buckets and bounds hashing work.\\n- Denied requests are not recorded as hits, so `Retry-After` is exactly ceil(seconds until the oldest recorded hit leaves the window), clamped to [1, window]; a 429 storm cannot extend a caller's penalty.\\n- Bucket eviction at MAX_TRACKED_KEYS (10000) never evicts `post:key:` buckets, matching the design's stated cardinality bound of MAX_TRACKED_KEYS + len(keys).\\n- Duplicate names in SHORTENER_API_KEYS: last entry wins.\\n\\nSecurity behaviour worth reviewing first:\\n- app/urls.py resolves every non-literal host via getaddrinfo and rejects the request when resolution fails. This is the safe direction but means creating a link requires working DNS for the target host; literal-IP targets need no DNS. Resolution verdicts are cached for 30s (max 1024 hosts) so a burst of creations does not repeat lookups.\\n- Only the stored, validated URL is ever used as a redirect destination; there is no code path where a query parameter, header or path segment becomes a redirect target.\\n- Settings.api_key_entries is declared `repr=False` so plaintext key names cannot appear in a dataclass repr or traceback frame; RateLimiter hashes them at construction with a per-boot 16-byte salt and keeps only digests.\\n\\nOther notes:\\n- requirements.txt pins the three libraries the constraints name, plus uvicorn (needed to actually run the ASGI app) and httpx/pytest (Starlette's TestClient requires httpx). No runtime import outside the pinned set.\\n- I did not add any files under tests/ because test authoring runs concurrently and I must not collide with the generated suite.\\n- SCHEMA_STATEMENTS creates exactly `links` and `clicks` plus the documented index on clicks.link_id; no table, column or index is added by the key limiter.\"}", "tier": "deep"}
